Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method quantitatively and qualitatively surpasses recent state-of-the-art methods. Notably, our approach achieves superior performance with computational complexity on par with existing methods. The source code and models are available at https://github.com/MingyuChoi-run/LSM

中文摘要翻译: 线性递归单元（LRU）基于稳定的线性递归原理设计，在长程依赖任务上展现了出色的准确性和鲁棒性。然而，其静态参数化和单扫描方式限制了其在2D视觉任务中的应用。在本研究中，我们提出了一种基于LRU的恢复网络，并引入语义调制单元（SMU），以在单图像超分辨率中实现性能与效率的和谐平衡。SMU承担三个关键角色：LRU调制、空间分类以及基于学习原型的特征增强。大量实验表明，我们的方法在定量和定性上均超越了近期最先进的方法。值得注意的是，我们的方法在计算复杂度与现有方法相当的情况下取得了更优的性能。源代码和模型已开源在https://github.com/MingyuChoi-run/LSM。

</details>


### [31] [Gaussian Process Prior Variational Autoencoder for Endoscopic Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19908)
*Ivan De Boi,Xinxing Shi,Xiaoyu Jiang,Tim J. M. Jaspers,Francisco Caetano,Mauricio A. Alvarez,Fons van der Sommen,Sam Van der Jeught*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于高斯过程先验变分自编码器（GPVAE）的内窥镜视频恢复框架，通过时序高斯过程先验替代标准因子化潜变量先验，实现缺失帧插值与不确定性感知重建，在结肠镜数据集上图像重建RMSE降低21.9%，下游轨迹RMSE降低12.7%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 内窥镜视频序列常受镜面反射、运动伪影和缺帧等瞬态损坏影响，降低图像可解释性并干扰下游任务（如3D重建与导航）。现有方法大多逐帧独立处理，未能充分利用视频的时序连续性，因此需要一种能利用时间依赖关系的恢复方法。

Method: 提出GPVAE框架，用时序高斯过程先验替代标准VAE的因子化潜变量先验，实现时序依赖建模与缺失帧插值。结合内窥镜专用编码器（卷积EndoVAE骨干和GastroNet-5M预训练Vision Transformer）与两种可扩展GP近似方法（层次先验近似HPA和稀疏精度近似SPA）。镜面反射通过DUCKNet掩码流水线处理，将受损像素排除在重建目标之外。

Result: 在C3VDv2结肠镜数据集上，最佳GPVAE变体相比匹配的VAE基线，图像重建RMSE平均降低21.9%（最高达26.1%）；下游轨迹RMSE在经典视觉里程计和预训练PoseNet上平均降低12.7%；训练时间每epoch平均增加27.3%。GP后验提供每帧不确定性估计，反映时序支撑程度并给出恢复帧的置信度信号。

Conclusion: GPVAE框架通过引入时序高斯过程先验有效提升了内窥镜视频恢复质量，在重建精度和下游任务性能上均显著优于标准VAE基线，且能提供不确定性估计作为恢复帧的置信度信号，为内窥镜视频恢复提供了一种有效的时序建模方案。

Original Abstract: Endoscopic video analysis is essential for gastrointestinal diagnosis and computer-assisted interventions, but video sequences are routinely degraded by specular reflections, motion artifacts, and missing frames. These transient corruptions can distract clinicians, reduce image interpretability, and disrupt downstream tasks such as 3D reconstruction and navigation. Effective restoration therefore requires methods that exploit temporal continuity rather than treating frames in isolation. We introduce a Gaussian Process Prior Variational Autoencoder (GPVAE) framework for endoscopic video restoration that replaces the standard factorized latent prior with a temporal Gaussian process prior, enabling interpolation of missing frames with uncertainty-aware reconstruction.
  The framework combines endoscopy-specific encoders, including a convolutional EndoVAE backbone and pretrained Vision Transformer encoders from GastroNet-5M, with two scalable GP approximations: Hierarchical Prior Approximation (HPA) and Sparse Precision Approximation (SPA). Specular reflections are handled using a DUCKNet-based masking pipeline that excludes corrupted pixels from the reconstruction objective. On the C3VDv2 colonoscopy dataset, the best GPVAE variants reduced image reconstruction RMSE by 21.9\% on average, and by up to 26.1\%, relative to matched VAE baselines. Downstream trajectory RMSE was reduced by 12.7\% on average across classical visual odometry and a pretrained PoseNet, at an average increase of 27.3\% in training time per epoch. Finally, the GP posterior provides per-frame uncertainty estimates that reflect temporal support and offer a confidence signal for restored frames.

中文摘要翻译: 内窥镜视频分析对于胃肠道诊断和计算机辅助干预至关重要，但视频序列常因镜面反射、运动伪影和缺失帧而退化。这些瞬态损坏会分散临床医生的注意力、降低图像可解释性，并干扰三维重建和导航等下游任务。因此，有效的恢复需要利用时序连续性的方法，而非逐帧独立处理。我们提出了一种基于高斯过程先验变分自编码器（GPVAE）的内窥镜视频恢复框架，该框架用时序高斯过程先验替代标准因子化潜变量先验，从而能够以不确定性感知的重建方式插值缺失帧。该框架结合了内窥镜专用编码器（包括卷积EndoVAE骨干网络和来自GastroNet-5M的预训练Vision Transformer编码器）与两种可扩展的GP近似方法：层次先验近似（HPA）和稀疏精度近似（SPA）。镜面反射通过基于DUCKNet的掩码流水线处理，将受损像素从重建目标中排除。在C3VDv2结肠镜数据集上，最佳GPVAE变体相比匹配的VAE基线，图像重建RMSE平均降低21.9%，最高降低26.1%。在下游任务中，经典视觉里程计和预训练PoseNet的轨迹RMSE平均降低12.7%，同时每epoch训练时间平均增加27.3%。最后，GP后验提供了每帧不确定性估计，该估计反映了时序支撑程度，并为恢复帧提供了置信度信号。

</details>


### [32] [SpatialSV: Internalizing Interpretable 3D Spatial Awareness in MLLMs via Task-Oriented Visual Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19915)
*Jiayu Tang,Yuchen Zhou,Chao Gou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 SpatialSV 框架，通过任务导向的视觉监督迫使多模态大语言模型将2D视觉特征显式提升为3D表示（深度图、相机位姿、点云），从而内化3D空间智能并提供可解释性，实验证明其有效性和半监督泛化能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法要么依赖外部工具注入空间先验（推理开销大），要么依赖隐层特征蒸馏（不可解释、缺乏细粒度几何约束），无法同时实现高效3D空间感知与内在可解释性。

Method: 提出 SpatialSV 框架，采用任务导向的视觉监督（而非被动特征模仿），迫使MLLM主动将2D视觉特征提升为显式3D表示（深度图、相机位姿、点云），该2D-to-3D提升过程提供透明窗口，用于可视化诊断模型内在空间知识质量。

Result: 在多个模型和基准上验证了 SpatialSV 提升和解释MLLM空间智能的有效性；在半监督设置下展现出强泛化能力，表明可利用无标注视觉数据进行可扩展的可解释空间表征学习。

Conclusion: SpatialSV 框架成功内化了MLLM的鲁棒3D空间感知能力，同时提供了内在可解释性，在半监督场景下具有良好泛化性，为可扩展的可解释空间表征学习开辟了新路径。

Original Abstract: Unlocking the spatial intelligence of multimodal large language model (MLLMs) is crucial for understanding and interacting with the 3D world. Prevailing approaches typically inject spatial priors via external tools, which impose significant inference overhead, or rely on latent feature distillation, which remains uninterpretable and lacks fine-grained geometric constraints. To address these issues, we propose SpatialSV, a framework designed to internalize robust 3D spatial awareness within MLLMs while simultaneously offering inherent interpretability. Deviating from passive feature imitation, SpatialSV employs task-oriented visual supervision, compelling the model to actively lift its 2D visual features into explicit 3D representations, including depth maps, camera poses, and point clouds. Crucially, this 2D-to-3D lifting process provides a transparent window into the model's representations: the resulting 3D reconstructions serve as an intuitive proxy for visualizing and diagnosing the quality of the model's intrinsic spatial knowledge. Extensive experiments across multiple models and benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of SpatialSV in enhancing and interpreting MLLMs' spatial intelligence. Furthermore, the framework exhibits strong generalization in semi-supervised settings, validating its potential to leverage unlabeled visual data for scalable, interpretable spatial representation learning.

中文摘要翻译: 释放多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的空间智能对于理解和交互3D世界至关重要。现有方法通常通过外部工具注入空间先验（带来显著推理开销），或依赖隐层特征蒸馏（缺乏可解释性且缺少细粒度几何约束）。为解决这些问题，我们提出 SpatialSV 框架，旨在让MLLM内化鲁棒的3D空间感知能力，同时提供内在可解释性。不同于被动的特征模仿，SpatialSV 采用任务导向的视觉监督，迫使模型主动将其2D视觉特征提升为显式3D表示，包括深度图、相机位姿和点云。关键在于，这一2D-to-3D提升过程为模型表示提供了透明窗口：生成的3D重建可作为直观的代理，用于可视化和诊断模型内在空间知识的质量。跨多个模型和基准的大量实验表明，SpatialSV 在增强和解释MLLM空间智能方面具有有效性。此外，该框架在半监督设置下展现出强泛化能力，验证了其利用无标注视觉数据进行可扩展、可解释的空间表征学习的潜力。

</details>


### [33] [CARE: Competence-Aware Reward Shaping for Adaptive Reasoning Length in Video-MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19927)
*Chengwen Liu,Hao Peng,Jisheng Dang,Hong Peng,Bin Hu,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CARE框架，通过能力感知的奖励塑形自适应优化多模态视频推理的推理长度，在提升推理准确率的同时显著提高token效率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的多模态视频推理方法采用简单固定的推理长度控制策略，无法适应模型能力动态变化——早期抑制必要探索，后期导致冗余推理和低效解码。

Method: CARE框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 通过指数移动平均维护平滑的能力估计，据此将训练分为渐进阶段，从探索导向的长推理逐步转向效率导向的简洁推理；(2) 利用批次级统计对推理努力进行归一化，避免冗长与任务内在复杂度混淆；(3) 引入后验放大器，增强历史困难样本上意外强表现的奖励信号。该机制无缝集成于GRPO训练管线，无额外推理开销。

Result: 在多个视频推理和通用视频理解基准上，CARE一致提升了推理准确率，稳定了强化学习训练，显著提高了token效率。训练中推理长度呈倒U型轨迹，收敛时产生更短但信息量更大的推理链，表明推理预算实现了有效自适应分配。

Conclusion: 能力感知的奖励塑形能够有效实现推理长度自适应优化，在保持甚至提升推理质量的同时大幅提升计算效率，为多模态推理的效率-准确性权衡提供了新范式。

Original Abstract: In multimodal video reasoning, reinforcement learning-based methods typically rely on simplistic and inflexible reasoning-length control strategies that fail to adapt to the model's evolving competence. This mismatch may suppress necessary exploration at early stages, while encouraging redundant reasoning and inefficient decoding once the model becomes more competent. In this paper, we propose CARE, a competence-aware reward shaping framework for adaptive reasoning length optimization in multimodal reasoning. Specifically, CARE maintains a smoothed competence estimate via an exponential moving average of pass rates, and uses it to route training into progressive stages that shift the reward preference from exploration-oriented long-form reasoning to efficiency-oriented concise reasoning. To avoid conflating verbosity with intrinsic task complexity, CARE further normalizes reasoning effort with batch-level statistics, and introduces a posterior amplifier to strengthen reward signals for unexpectedly strong performance on historically difficult samples. The proposed mechanism is seamlessly integrated into the GRPO training pipeline and incurs no additional inference-time overhead. Extensive experiments on multiple video reasoning and general video understanding benchmarks demonstrate that CARE consistently improves reasoning accuracy, stabilizes reinforcement learning, and significantly enhances token efficiency. Moreover, CARE exhibits a characteristic inverted-U trajectory of reasoning length during training, and yields shorter yet more informative reasoning traces at convergence, indicating effective adaptive allocation of reasoning budget. We provide the source code for our proposed CARE framework and experiments at https://github.com/1Pansy/Video-CARE.

中文摘要翻译: 在多模态视频推理中，基于强化学习的方法通常依赖简单且不灵活的推理长度控制策略，无法适应模型不断变化的能力。这种不匹配可能在早期阶段抑制必要的探索，而在模型能力增强后则鼓励冗余推理和低效解码。本文提出CARE，一种用于多模态推理中自适应推理长度优化的能力感知奖励塑形框架。具体而言，CARE通过指数移动平均维护一个平滑的能力估计，并据此将训练分为渐进阶段，逐步将奖励偏好从探索导向的长形式推理转向效率导向的简洁推理。为避免将冗长与内在任务复杂度混淆，CARE进一步利用批次级统计对推理努力进行归一化，并引入后验放大器来增强在历史困难样本上出现意外强表现时的奖励信号。该机制无缝集成到GRPO训练管线中，且不引入额外的推理开销。在多个视频推理和通用视频理解基准上的大量实验表明，CARE一致提升了推理准确率，稳定了强化学习训练，并显著提高了token效率。此外，CARE在训练过程中展现出特征性的倒U型推理长度轨迹，并在收敛时产生更短但信息量更大的推理链，表明推理预算实现了有效的自适应分配。我们在https://github.com/1Pansy/Video-CARE上提供了CARE框架及实验的源代码。

</details>


### [34] [Spatial-Aware Reduction Framework: Towards Efficient and Faithful Visual State Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19932)
*Jindi Lv,Aoyu Li,Yuhao Zhou,Zheng Zhu,Xiaofeng Wang,Qing Ye,Yueqi Duan,Wentao Feng,Jiancheng Lv*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出STORM，一种空间感知的令牌缩减框架，在不训练的情况下保持视觉Mamba模型的结构完整性，解决了现有缩减方法在结构化Mamba变体中的性能崩溃问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有令牌缩减方法在应用于结构增强的Mamba变体时，会导致严重的性能崩溃。作者将此归因于现有缩减方法的空间不可知特性，破坏了选择性扫描机制所需的二维结构前提。

Method: STORM将令牌缩减重新表述为空间单元上的结构化操作，通过施加局部约束来保持网格拓扑和邻域连贯性。作为一个即插即用模块，无需训练即可赋予现有缩减管道显式的空间感知能力。

Result: 在无训练设置下，STORM在多种视觉Mamba骨干网络上实现了最优剪枝精度。在VMamba上准确率恢复高达63.3%（top-1），在PlainMamba上仅下降1.0%的准确率，性能与ViT相当。

Conclusion: 通过引入空间感知的令牌缩减策略，STORM有效解决了Mamba模型在令牌压缩中的结构破坏问题，实现了高效且保持性能的视觉建模。

Original Abstract: Mamba demonstrates strong efficiency in modeling long visual sequences. However, when token reduction is applied to structurally enhanced Mamba variants, these models exhibit a severe performance collapse. We attribute this degradation to the spatially agnostic nature of existing reduction methods, which violate the two-dimensional structural premise required by the selective scanning mechanism. In this work, we propose STORM, a spatial-aware token reduction framework designed to maintain structural integrity throughout the compression process. STORM reformulates reduction into a structured operation on spatial units, enforcing localized constraints to maintain both grid topology and neighborhood coherence. As a plug-and-play module, STORM equips existing reduction pipelines with explicit spatial awareness without any training. Empirical results demonstrate that STORM achieves state-of-the-art pruning accuracy across diverse vision Mamba backbones under training-free settings. Notably, STORM delivers a substantial accuracy recovery on VMamba, outperforming prior methods by up to 63.3\% in top-1 accuracy. Meanwhile, STORM incurs only a 1.0\% accuracy drop on PlainMamba, achieving performance comparable to ViT.

中文摘要翻译: Mamba在长视觉序列建模中展现了高效的性能。然而，当令牌缩减应用于结构增强的Mamba变体时，这些模型会出现严重的性能崩溃。我们将这种退化归因于现有缩减方法的空间不可知特性，这些方法破坏了选择性扫描机制所需的二维结构前提。在本工作中，我们提出STORM，一种空间感知的令牌缩减框架，旨在压缩过程中保持结构完整性。STORM将令牌缩减重新表述为空间单元上的结构化操作，通过施加局部约束来维持网格拓扑和邻域连贯性。作为一个即插即用模块，STORM无需任何训练即可赋予现有缩减管道显式的空间感知能力。实验结果表明，在无训练设置下，STORM在多种视觉Mamba骨干网络上实现了最优的剪枝精度。值得注意的是，STORM在VMamba上实现了显著的准确率恢复，top-1准确率相比先前方法提升高达63.3%。同时，STORM在PlainMamba上仅造成1.0%的准确率下降，达到了与ViT相当的性能。

</details>


### [35] [Speeding up the annotation process in semantic segmentation industrial applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19934)
*Marta Fernandez-Moreno,Margarita Guerrero,Rosalia Rementeria,Pablo Mesejo,Raul Moreno*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次量化了无监督算法在工业材料科学语义分割标注任务中的加速效果，证明可将标注时间从170小时降至37小时（减少78%），并发布了迄今最大的公开钢微观结构分割数据集及基准深度学习模型。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 数据标注是机器学习流程中的瓶颈，尤其在复杂的语义分割任务（如材料科学中的微观结构表征）中，人工标注耗时且易出错。现有研究虽量化了标注时间或探索了无监督方法，但尚未量化无监督算法对标注过程的加速程度。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 利用无监督计算机视觉算法作为预标注步骤，辅助高分辨率图像（1280×959和960×703像素）的像素级语义分割标注；对比从零开始标注与使用无监督算法预标注后的标注时间；训练深度学习模型并在工业场景中部署验证。

Result: 标注时间从170小时降至37小时，减少约78%；创建了迄今最大的公开钢微观结构分割数据集（MIT许可，永久DOI）；提供了经领域专家验证并在工业场景中部署的深度学习基准模型。

Conclusion: 无监督算法能显著加速复杂语义分割任务的标注流程，大幅降低人工成本，同时保证标注质量。该数据集和模型为材料科学领域提供了重要的公共基准资源。

Original Abstract: Current machine learning models commonly require large and well-annotated datasets. However, the annotation process often becomes a bottleneck, with increased complexity leading to higher chances of human errors. Within this context, our goal in this paper is to leverage unsupervised algorithms to improve data annotation efficiency for complex semantic segmentation problems in industrial materials science. Previous research has quantified labeling time and others explored unsupervised methods. However, to the best of our knowledge, this is the first study to quantify how much unsupervised algorithms accelerate the labeling process. We aim to validate the extent to which this laborious process can be accelerated, focusing on semantic segmentation tasks that involve annotating each pixel of high-resolution images, such as the microstructure characterization challenge in materials science. Specifically, we demonstrate that by using unsupervised computer vision algorithms, the time required for the labeling process can be reduced from 170 hours to 37 hours, achieving an approximate reduction of 78\%. The dataset we work with includes large images of dimensions 1280x959 and 960x703, which further increases the complexity of the annotation task. Despite these challenges, we create and share the largest public steel microstructure segmentation dataset to date, available under MIT License with permanent DOI, contributing a fully annotated, high-resolution dataset to the field. Additionally, this is the first work to compare the labeling time from scratch (a common approach in previous studies) to the labeling time when using these unsupervised algorithms as a pre-annotation step. Furthermore, we provide a Deep Learning model trained on this dataset, validated by field experts, and deployed in an industrial setting, serving as an initial benchmark for this public dataset.

中文摘要翻译: 当前的机器学习模型通常需要大规模且高质量标注的数据集。然而，标注过程常常成为瓶颈，随着复杂度的增加，人为错误的可能性也随之上升。在此背景下，本文旨在利用无监督算法提升工业材料科学中复杂语义分割问题的数据标注效率。以往的研究量化了标注时间，另一些工作探索了无监督方法。但据我们所知，这是首次量化无监督算法在多大程度上加速标注流程的研究。我们旨在验证这一繁琐过程能被加速的程度，重点关注需要对高分辨率图像进行逐像素标注的语义分割任务，例如材料科学中的微观结构表征挑战。具体而言，我们证明，通过使用无监督计算机视觉算法，标注所需时间可从170小时减少至37小时，实现了约78%的缩减。我们使用的数据集包含1280×959和960×703像素的大尺寸图像，这进一步增加了标注任务的复杂性。尽管存在这些挑战，我们创建并共享了迄今为止最大的公开钢微观结构分割数据集，该数据集在MIT许可下提供并拥有永久DOI，为领域贡献了一个完全标注的高分辨率数据集。此外，这是首个将从零开始标注（以往研究中的常见方法）与使用无监督算法作为预标注步骤的标注时间进行比较的工作。我们还提供了一个基于该数据集训练的深度学习模型，该模型经过领域专家验证，并已部署于工业场景，可作为该公共数据集的初始基准。

</details>


### [36] [Triangular Consistency as a Universal Constraint for Learning Optical Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19938)
*Yi Xiao,Carlos Rodriguez Coronel,Jing Zhan,Haniyeh Ehsani Oskouie,Alex Wong,Dong Lao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出三角一致性（Triangular Consistency）作为光流计算的首要原理约束，无需额外标注，可作为即插即用模块提升多种监督和设置下的光流估计性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有光流方法缺乏统一、通用的几何约束，且不同监督设置（有监督、无监督、迁移学习）下的训练目标各异，亟需一种与网络架构、监督类型和数据集无关的通用约束来提升光流估计的一致性和泛化能力。

Method: 提出三角一致性约束：通过组合两个光流场来推导出第三个光流场，并强制三者之间保持一致。具体包括三种形式：(i) 图像对之间的循环一致性；(ii) 多帧视频帧通过时间链式组合产生长程运动一致性；(iii) 图像对与可控合成变换相结合，实现数据增强。该方法作为即插即用模块，计算开销可忽略，无需额外标注。

Result: 在有监督、无监督和迁移学习等多种设置下，实验均取得了一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 三角一致性是从光流几何性质直接导出的通用约束，不依赖于模型特定假设，可作为通用的即插即用组件，有效提升光流训练效果。

Original Abstract: We propose triangular consistency as a first-principled constraint for optical flow, which is agnostic to network architecture, supervision type, and dataset, and applies to both image-pair and multi-frame settings. This simple but powerful constraint is to compose two flows to induce a third flow and enforce consistency among the three. The composed flows may arise from (i) image pairs, yielding cycle consistency; (ii) multiple video frames, producing longer-range motion through temporal chaining; or (iii) image pairs combined with controlled synthetic transformations, which becomes data augmentation. This triangular consistency introduces negligible computational overhead and requires no additional annotations. Since it is derived directly from the geometry of optical flow, it does not rely on model-specific assumptions and serves as a ``universal'' plug-and-play component for optical flow training. Experiments show consistent improvement across supervised, unsupervised, and transfer learning settings.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出将三角一致性作为光流计算的首要原理约束。该约束与网络架构、监督类型及数据集无关，且同时适用于图像对和多帧设置。这一简单而强大的约束通过组合两个光流场来推导出第三个光流场，并强制三者之间保持一致。组合的光流可来源于：(i) 图像对，产生循环一致性；(ii) 多帧视频帧，通过时间链式组合实现更长程的运动估计；(iii) 图像对与受控的合成变换相结合，实现数据增强。该三角一致性引入的计算开销可忽略不计，且无需任何额外标注。由于该约束直接源自光流的几何性质，不依赖模型特定的假设，因此可作为光流训练的"通用"即插即用组件。实验表明，在有监督、无监督和迁移学习等多种设置下，该方法均能取得一致的性能提升。

</details>


### [37] [DiffMath: Symbol- and Graph-Aware Latent Diffusion Transformer for Handwritten Mathematical Expression Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19939)
*Wei Pan,Xuhan Zheng,Yilin Shi,Huiguo He,Hiuyi Cheng,Dezhi Peng,Minghui Liao,Lianwen Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DiffMath，一种符号与图感知的潜在扩散框架，通过引入RelAST树结构表示、MathVAE结构保持编码器和MathDiT条件去噪模块，在无需位置监督的情况下实现高质量手写数学表达式生成，并在结构一致性和下游OCR增强方面超越现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有手写数学表达式生成方法依赖符号级边界框等显式空间监督，标注成本高且可扩展性差。本文旨在消除位置监督需求，利用LaTeX固有的层次结构作为先验知识，实现高效、可扩展的HMEG。

Method: (1) 设计Relational Abstract Syntax Tree (RelAST)，将MathML树蒸馏为紧凑三元组序列[S, R, D]，每个token编码符号身份、空间关系或嵌套深度；(2) 提出MathVAE，通过符号感知和关系感知的感知正则化学习保持结构信息的潜在表示；(3) 提出MathDiT，在结构化潜在空间中进行条件去噪，并利用自适应层归一化(AdaLN)引入全局符号计数先验以增强结构一致性。

Result: 实验表明DiffMath生成的表达式结构一致性更好，性能优于现有方法，且通过合成数据增强能提升下游OCR模型的准确率。

Conclusion: DiffMath证明了在无需显式位置监督的条件下，利用层次化结构先验结合扩散模型能够有效生成结构一致的手写数学表达式，并具有良好的下游应用价值。

Original Abstract: Handwritten Mathematical Expression Generation (HMEG) is challenging due to the complex two-dimensional layouts and long-range structural dependencies of mathematical expressions. Existing methods typically rely on explicit spatial supervision, such as symbol-level bounding boxes, which incurs high annotation costs and limits scalability. In this work, we propose DiffMath, a symbol- and graph-aware latent diffusion framework that leverages the hierarchical structure inherent in LaTeX as a structural prior, eliminating the need for positional supervision. First, we design a Relational Abstract Syntax Tree (RelAST), a generation-oriented representation that distills MathML trees into compact triplet sequences [S, R, D], where each token directly encodes a symbol identity, spatial relation, or nesting depth. Second, we introduce MathVAE, which learns structure-preserving latent representations through symbol-aware and relation-aware perceptual regularization, ensuring that the latent space captures both character semantics and spatial topology. Third, MathDiT performs conditional denoising in this structured latent space, further guided by a global symbol-count prior via Adaptive Layer Normalization (AdaLN) to improve structural coherence. Experiments show that DiffMath produces structurally consistent handwritten expressions, achieves superior performance over existing methods, and improves the accuracy of downstream OCR models through synthetic data augmentation.

中文摘要翻译: 手写数学表达式生成（HMEG）因数学表达式复杂的二维布局和长程结构依赖而极具挑战性。现有方法通常依赖显式空间监督（如符号级边界框），这导致标注成本高昂且限制了可扩展性。本文提出DiffMath，一种符号与图感知的潜在扩散框架，利用LaTeX中固有的层次结构作为结构先验，从而消除了位置监督的需求。首先，我们设计了关系抽象语法树（RelAST），一种面向生成的表示方法，将MathML树蒸馏为紧凑的三元组序列[S, R, D]，其中每个token直接编码符号身份、空间关系或嵌套深度。其次，我们引入MathVAE，通过符号感知和关系感知的感知正则化学习结构保持的潜在表示，确保潜在空间同时捕捉字符语义和空间拓扑。第三，MathDiT在该结构化潜在空间中执行条件去噪，并进一步通过自适应层归一化（AdaLN）引入全局符号计数先验以增强结构连贯性。实验表明，DiffMath能够生成结构一致的手写表达式，性能优于现有方法，并通过合成数据增强提升了下游OCR模型的准确率。

</details>


### [38] [Timage: A Generative Text-in-Image Paradigm for Fine-Tuning Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19944)
*Yifeng Wu,Huimin Huang,Ruiluo Wu,Chunyi Lin,Guanhua Chen,Xian Wu,Wang Song,Ruize Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Timage通过将文本查询直接绘制到图像上作为覆盖层，将多模态理解重构为输入层的对齐问题，利用约束薛定谔桥（cSB）生成布局和外观，从而在不修改模型权重的情况下显著提升细粒度空间推理性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在细粒度空间推理中经常丢失正确的图像区域，因为文本查询缺乏明确的几何锚点；现有方法（调整权重或填充冗长指令）无法可靠地将语言与视觉坐标对齐，且可能损害模型通用能力。

Method: 提出Timage范式，将查询作为排版覆盖层直接绘制到图像上。覆盖层的放置和外观由约束薛定谔桥（cSB）生成，这是一个熵最优传输采样器，将布局合成分解为两个耦合的随机阶段：第一阶段（区域搜索）在保护前景内容的同时，将噪声向查询对齐的图像区域传输；第二阶段（外观塑造）通过“墨水预算”正则化器调整字形大小，确保文本清晰且视觉平衡。

Result: 在VMCBench基准测试上，Timage搭配7B规模的基础模型，性能显著超越更大的专有系统以及参数调优的基线方法。

Conclusion: 有意识的输入重构是一种强大且架构中立的杠杆，能够加强多模态推理能力。

Original Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) often lose track of the right image regions during fine-grained spatial reasoning, because a textual query rarely carries any explicit geometric anchor into the pixel domain. Prevailing remedies either rewire the model's weights or pad the prompt with verbose instructions, yet neither reliably pins the language to the correct visual coordinates without eroding the backbone's general competence. We introduce Timage, a paradigm that recasts multimodal understanding as an alignment problem solved at the input: the query is drawn, as a typeset overlay, onto the image itself. The placement and appearance of this overlay are produced by a Constrained Schrödinger Bridge (cSB), an entropic optimal-transport sampler that factorizes layout synthesis into two coupled stochastic stages. The first stage, Region Search, transports noise toward query-aligned image zones while obeying a hard occlusion barrier that protects salient foreground content; the second stage, Appearance Shaping, sizes the glyphs through an ``ink-budget'' regularizer so that the rendered text stays legible and visually balanced. The resulting overlay behaves as an explicit attention beacon that channels the model's focus along spatial semantics. On the VMCBench suite, Timage paired with a modest 7B backbone clearly overtakes far larger proprietary systems as well as parameter-tuned baselines. The study positions deliberate input reconstruction as a powerful, architecture-neutral lever for strengthening multimodal reasoning.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在细粒度空间推理中常常丢失正确的图像区域，因为文本查询很少携带任何明确的几何锚点进入像素域。现有的补救措施要么重新调整模型权重，要么用冗长的指令填充提示词，但两者都无法在不妨碍骨干模型通用能力的情况下，可靠地将语言锚定到正确的视觉坐标。我们提出Timage，一种将多模态理解重新构想为在输入层解决的对齐问题的新范式：查询被作为排版覆盖层直接绘制到图像上。该覆盖层的放置和外观由约束薛定谔桥（cSB）生成，这是一种熵最优传输采样器，将布局合成分解为两个耦合的随机阶段。第一阶段，区域搜索，将噪声向查询对齐的图像区域传输，同时遵循一个硬性遮挡屏障以保护显著的前景内容；第二阶段，外观塑造，通过“墨水预算”正则化器调整字形大小，使得渲染的文本保持清晰且视觉平衡。由此产生的覆盖层充当一个显式的注意力信标，引导模型的焦点沿空间语义分布。在VMCBench基准测试上，Timage搭配一个适中的7B骨干模型，明显超越了远为更大的专有系统以及参数调优的基线方法。该研究将有意识的输入重构定位为一种强大且架构中立的杠杆，用于加强多模态推理。

</details>


### [39] [Confidence Calibration for Multimodal LLMs: An Empirical Study through Medical VQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19950)
*Yuetian Du,Yucheng Wang,Ming Kong,Tian Liang,Qiang Long,Bingdi Chen,Qiang Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在医疗任务中置信度与准确率错位的问题，提出了一种结合多策略融合式询问（MS-FBI）与辅助专家大语言模型评估的置信度校准方法，在三个医学VQA数据集上将期望校准误差（ECE）平均降低40%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在医疗任务中表现良好，但其输出的置信度往往与实际准确率不一致，这可能导致误诊或忽略正确建议。现有研究缺乏对医疗MLLMs中准确率与置信度关系的系统分析，亟需针对医疗领域进行置信度校准。

Method: 提出一种结合多策略融合式询问（Multi-Strategy Fusion-Based Interrogation, MS-FBI）与辅助专家大语言模型评估的置信度校准方法，用于医学视觉问答（Medical VQA）任务。

Result: 在三个医学VQA数据集上，该方法将期望校准误差（Expected Calibration Error, ECE）平均降低40%，显著提升了MLLMs的可靠性。

Conclusion: 医疗领域特定的置信度校准对于提升多模态大语言模型在AI辅助诊断中的可信度和可靠性至关重要，本文为更可信的AI辅助诊断提供了可行方案。

Original Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) show great potential in medical tasks, but their elicited confidence often misaligns with actual accuracy, potentially leading to misdiagnosis or overlooking correct advice. This study presents the first comprehensive analysis of the relationship between accuracy and confidence in medical MLLMs. It proposes a novel method that combines Multi-Strategy Fusion-Based Interrogation (MS-FBI) with auxiliary expert LLM assessment, aiming to improve confidence calibration in Medical Visual Question Answering (VQA). Experiments demonstrate that our method reduces the Expected Calibration Error (ECE) by an average of 40\% across three Medical VQA datasets, significantly enhancing MLLMs' reliability. The findings highlight the importance of domain-specific calibration for MLLMs in healthcare, offering a more trustworthy solution for AI-assisted diagnosis.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在医疗任务中展现出巨大潜力，但其输出的置信度往往与实际准确率不一致，可能导致误诊或忽略正确的建议。本研究首次对医疗MLLMs中准确率与置信度之间的关系进行了全面分析。本文提出了一种新颖方法，将多策略融合式询问（MS-FBI）与辅助专家大语言模型评估相结合，旨在提升医学视觉问答（VQA）中的置信度校准效果。实验表明，该方法在三个医学VQA数据集上将期望校准误差（ECE）平均降低了40%，显著增强了MLLMs的可靠性。研究结果强调了在医疗保健领域对MLLMs进行领域特定校准的重要性，为AI辅助诊断提供了更可信的解决方案。

</details>


### [40] [SketchKeyAnime: Reference-anchored Sparse Key-Sketch Animation Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19958)
*Meixi Li,Xianlin Zhang,Yue Zhang,Xueming Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SketchKeyAnime，一种基于视频扩散的动画生成框架，仅需单张参考RGB图像和少量关键草图即可生成结构可控、外观一致且时间连贯的动画，在Sakuga-42M数据集上显著优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统动画制作依赖大量人工绘制与迭代细化，现有动画和视频生成方法通常需要RGB边界帧、密集帧级条件或完整草图序列，在低成本输入条件下适用性受限。

Method: 提出SketchKeyAnime框架，采用双分支条件机制编码局部几何约束与语义时间上下文；通过Sketch Cross Attention融合参考图像和草图条件并引入可学习门控；设计自适应加权损失以加强对关键草图的监督。

Result: 在Sakuga-42M的美学子集上，相比最优基线，EDMD降低31.9%，FVD降低9.5%，在草图保真度和时间连贯性上表现最佳，大部分定量指标取得最优。

Conclusion: SketchKeyAnime验证了从稀疏关键草图输入生成高质量动画的可行性，展现了低成本、高可控动画创作的潜力。

Original Abstract: Traditional animation production relies heavily on manual drawing and iterative refinement, particularly for key-pose design, in-betweening, and character coloring. While existing animation and video generation methods have made notable progress, they typically depend on RGB boundary frames, dense frame-wise conditions, or complete sketch sequences, limiting their applicability under low-cost input conditions. We present SketchKeyAnime, a video diffusion framework for generating structurally controllable, appearance-consistent, and temporally coherent animations from sparse key-sketch inputs. Given a single reference RGB image and a few temporally indexed key sketches, SketchKeyAnime introduces a dual-branch conditioning mechanism to encode local geometric constraints alongside semantic-temporal context. It leverages Sketch Cross Attention to fuse reference image and sketch conditions with learnable gating, and incorporates an Adaptive Weighted Loss to strengthen supervision on key-sketch frames and line-art regions. Experimental results on the Aesthetic subset of Sakuga-42M show that our approach consistently outperforms representative animation interpolation and sketch-guided generation baselines. Compared to the best-performing baseline, SketchKeyAnime reduces EDMD by 31.9\% and FVD by 9.5\%, demonstrating superior sketch fidelity and temporal coherence, while achieving the best overall performance across most quantitative metrics. These results validate the proposed framework and highlight its potential for low-cost, highly controllable animation creation.

中文摘要翻译: 传统动画制作严重依赖人工绘制和迭代细化，尤其是在关键姿势设计、中间帧生成和角色着色方面。尽管现有的动画和视频生成方法已取得显著进展，但它们通常依赖于RGB边界帧、密集的帧级条件或完整的草图序列，这限制了它们在低成本输入条件下的适用性。我们提出SketchKeyAnime，一种视频扩散框架，用于从稀疏的关键草图输入中生成结构可控、外观一致且时间连贯的动画。给定一张参考RGB图像和少量带时间索引的关键草图，SketchKeyAnime引入了一种双分支条件机制，以编码局部几何约束以及语义时间上下文。它利用Sketch Cross Attention将参考图像和草图条件与可学习门控相融合，并引入自适应加权损失以加强对关键草图帧和线条艺术区域的监督。在Sakuga-42M美学子集上的实验结果表明，我们的方法在代表性动画插值和草图引导生成基线中表现一致更优。与最佳基线相比，SketchKeyAnime将EDMD降低了31.9%，FVD降低了9.5%，在草图保真度和时间连贯性方面表现出色，并在大多数定量指标上取得了最佳整体性能。这些结果验证了所提出框架的有效性，并突显了其在低成本、高可控动画创作方面的潜力。

</details>


### [41] [Addressing Detail Bottlenecks in Latent Diffusion for RGB-to-SWIR Image Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19961)
*Kaili Wang,Martin Dimitrievski,Jose Maria Salvador,Ben Stoffelen,David Van Hamme,Lore Goetschalckx*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对潜扩散模型在图像到图像翻译中丢失空间细节的问题，提出两种轻量级改进：源条件自编码器（SCAE）和可学习引导编码器（LGE），在RGB到SWIR翻译任务中将检测mAP提升最多2倍，小目标检测提升最多3.4倍，并实现SOTA的FID。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 潜扩散模型在压缩过程中丢弃了精细空间细节，导致下游感知任务性能下降。作者识别出两个瓶颈：自编码器丢失空间信息，以及条件路径通过朴素下采样进一步退化源信号。

Method: 提出两种与主干网络无关的轻量级改进：(1) 源条件自编码器（SCAE），通过跳跃连接将高分辨率源特征注入解码器；(2) 可学习引导编码器（LGE），用可学习的条件信号替代朴素下采样。

Result: 在RGB到SWIR翻译任务上，使用U-Net和DiT两种去噪器主干，检测mAP比潜扩散基线提升最多2倍，小目标（COCO-small, <32² px²）检测提升最多3.4倍，同时实现SOTA的FID。FID与检测性能之间相关性较弱，支持多轴评估。方法零样本泛化到公开RASMD基准。

Conclusion: 所提出的SCAE和LGE能有效恢复潜扩散模型中丢失的空间细节，显著提升下游检测任务性能，且FID与感知任务性能弱相关，需多维度评估。

Original Abstract: Latent diffusion models (LDMs) enable efficient image-to-image translation but discard fine spatial details during compression, degrading downstream perception tasks. We identify two bottlenecks: the autoencoder, which loses spatial information, and the conditioning pathway, which further degrades the source signal through naive downsampling. We propose two lightweight, backbone-agnostic fixes: a Source-Conditioned Autoencoder (SCAE) that injects high-resolution source features into the decoder via skip connections, and a Learnable Guidance Encoder (LGE) that replaces naive downsampling with a learned conditioning signal. Evaluated on RGB-to-SWIR translation for driving scenes with two denoiser backbones (U-Net and DiT), our approach improves detection mAP by up to 2x over the latent diffusion baseline, with up to 3.4x gains on small objects (COCO-small, <32^2 px^2), while achieving state-of-the-art FID. We further show that FID and detection performance are poorly correlated, motivating multi-axis evaluation. Results generalise zero-shot to the public RASMD benchmark. We will publicly release test data with annotations, all checkpoints, and training code.

中文摘要翻译: 潜扩散模型（LDMs）能够实现高效的图像到图像翻译，但在压缩过程中丢弃了精细的空间细节，从而降低了下游感知任务的性能。我们识别出两个瓶颈：自编码器丢失空间信息，以及条件路径通过朴素下采样进一步退化源信号。我们提出两种轻量级、与主干网络无关的改进方法：源条件自编码器（SCAE），通过跳跃连接将高分辨率源特征注入解码器；以及可学习引导编码器（LGE），用可学习的条件信号替代朴素下采样。在驾驶场景的RGB到SWIR翻译任务上，使用两种去噪器主干（U-Net和DiT）进行评估，我们的方法将检测mAP比潜扩散基线提升最多2倍，小目标（COCO-small, <32²像素²）检测提升最多3.4倍，同时实现了最先进的FID。我们进一步表明FID与检测性能之间相关性较弱，这支持多轴评估的必要性。结果在公共RASMD基准上实现了零样本泛化。我们将公开发布带标注的测试数据、所有检查点和训练代码。

</details>


### [42] [ROSE: Benchmarking the Perception-to-Action Gap in Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19965)
*Yihao Wang,Zijian He,Jie Ren,Keze Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ROSE基准，通过固定视觉场景但改变任务上下文（计数vs.区域条件动作），评估多模态大模型将相同视觉证据转化为不同上下文所需动作的能力。实验发现模型性能从计数任务到区域条件动作任务下降高达44.5个百分点，揭示了模型在将共享视觉证据转化为上下文特定动作时存在独立于坐标定位的瓶颈。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有MLLMs在处理相同视觉场景时，可能因任务上下文不同而需要执行不同动作，但缺乏一个控制视觉场景固定、仅改变任务条件（区域约束和符号输出）的基准来系统评估模型能否可靠地将相同视觉证据转化为当前上下文所需的动作。

Method: 提出ROSE（Reference-conditioned Oddity and Symbolic Execution）基准，通过耦合计数任务和坐标动作任务，固定视觉场景不变，同时改变区域约束和要求的符号输出，测试模型是否能在变化上下文下推断隐式多数参考并基于细粒度视觉证据执行动作。

Result: 在九个近期MLLMs上，从计数导向任务到区域条件动作任务，性能下降高达44.5个百分点，而人类表现达98.8%。该差距在模型能正确计数的配对场景和区域上仍然存在。全局点击和匹配局部控制实验表明，坐标定位仅能解释部分损失，揭示了模型在将共享视觉证据转化为上下文特定动作时存在独特的、依赖于模型的瓶颈。

Conclusion: 多模态大模型在将相同视觉证据灵活地转化为不同上下文所需动作方面存在显著且系统性的不足，这种能力缺陷独立于基本的视觉定位能力，是一个需要专门关注的模型瓶颈。

Original Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are increasingly expected to act on visual information, yet the same scene may require different actions under different task contexts. How reliably can a model turn the same visual evidence into the action required by the current context? To answer this question, we introduce \textsc{ROSE} (\textbf{R}eference-conditioned \textbf{O}ddity and \textbf{S}ymbolic \textbf{E}xecution), a controlled benchmark that holds the visual scene fixed while varying region constraints and required symbolic outputs. Through coupled counting and coordinate-action tasks, \textsc{ROSE} tests whether models can infer an implicit majority reference and act on the resulting fine-grained visual evidence under changing contexts. Across nine recent MLLMs, performance drops by as much as 44.5 percentage points from counting-oriented tasks to region-conditioned action, despite 98.8\% human performance. The gap persists on paired scenes and regions for which the same model returns the correct count, while global-click and matched local controls show that coordinate grounding explains only part of the loss, revealing a distinct, model-dependent bottleneck in turning shared visual evidence into context-specific actions.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）日益被期望基于视觉信息采取行动，然而相同的场景在不同任务上下文中可能需要不同的行动。模型能否可靠地将相同的视觉证据转化为当前上下文所需的行动？为回答此问题，我们引入了\textsc{ROSE}（基于参考的异常检测与符号执行），这是一个受控基准，它在固定视觉场景的同时改变区域约束和所需符号输出。通过耦合的计数与坐标动作任务，\textsc{ROSE}测试模型是否能在变化的上下文中推断隐式的多数参考，并基于由此产生的细粒度视觉证据采取行动。在九个近期MLLMs上，从计数导向任务到区域条件动作，性能下降高达44.5个百分点，而人类表现达98.8%。在模型能正确返回计数的配对场景和区域上，该差距依然存在。全局点击和匹配局部控制实验表明，坐标定位仅能解释部分损失，从而揭示了模型在将共享视觉证据转化为上下文特定动作时存在一个独特的、依赖于模型的瓶颈。

</details>


### [43] [Semantic-Anchored Evidential Fusion for Domain-Robust Whole-Slide Survival Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19966)
*Yucheng Xing,Ling Huang,Pei Liu,Jingying Ma,Jiaqing Xu,Kai He,Mengling Feng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SAEFS框架，利用VQA提取语义锚点，结合不确定建模与谨慎融合规则，实现跨中心零样本癌症预后，平均C-index提升10.2%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有WSI预后方法主要关注域内性能，因依赖像素级特征而易受染色协议和扫描硬件等域特定伪影影响，无法跨临床中心泛化。

Method: SAEFS框架通过VQA从WSI中推导语义锚点，采用双流WSI证据提取架构，利用基于Dirichlet的主观逻辑建模不确定性，并通过谨慎合取规则融合语义与视觉证据，避免相关源的过置信融合。

Result: 仅在单一源域训练，在四个未见域零样本评估，SAEFS在预测准确性和可靠性上均一致优于现有方法，平均C-index提升10.2%；VQA语义特征的跨中心散度显著低于像素级特征。

Conclusion: 高层病理语义（如肿瘤分级、微环境结构）提供域不变语义表征，可模拟病理专家的稳健诊断逻辑，从而有效提升跨中心临床应用的鲁棒性。

Original Abstract: Whole-slide images (WSIs) are widely used for computational cancer prognosis. However, most existing methods primarily focus on in-domain performance and fail to generalize across clinical centers. This limitation stems from their reliance on pixel-derived representations that are highly susceptible to domain-specific artifacts caused by staining protocols and scanner hardware. We hypothesize that high-level pathology semantics, such as tumor grade and micro-environmental architecture, provide a domain-invariant semantic representation that mirrors the robust diagnostic logic of human pathologists. Therefore, we propose a Semantic-Anchored Evidential Fusion Survival (SAEFS) framework, where SAEFS derives semantic anchors from WSIs via Visual Question Answering (VQA), employs a dual-stream WSI evidence extraction architecture, uses Dirichlet-based Subjective Logic to model uncertainty, and fuses semantic and visual evidence through a cautious conjunction rule to avoid overconfident fusion from correlated sources. Trained exclusively on one source domain and evaluated zero-shot across four unseen domains, SAEFS consistently outperforms state-of-the-art models both in prediction accuracy and reliability, improving the average C-index by 10.2%. Quantitative analyses further show that VQA-derived semantic features exhibit significantly lower cross-center divergence than pixel-derived features, highlighting their robustness for cross-center clinical applications.

中文摘要翻译: 全切片图像（WSI）广泛应用于计算癌症预后。然而，现有方法主要关注域内性能，无法泛化至不同临床中心。这一局限源于它们依赖像素级表征，而此类表征极易受染色协议和扫描硬件等域特定伪影影响。我们假设高层病理语义（如肿瘤分级和微环境结构）可提供域不变的语义表征，模拟人类病理学家的稳健诊断逻辑。因此，我们提出语义锚定证据融合生存（SAEFS）框架。SAEFS通过视觉问答（VQA）从WSI中推导语义锚点，采用双流WSI证据提取架构，利用基于Dirichlet的主观逻辑建模不确定性，并通过谨慎合取规则融合语义与视觉证据，以避免来自相关源的过置信融合。仅在单一源域训练，在四个未见域上进行零样本评估，SAEFS在预测准确性和可靠性上均一致优于现有模型，平均C-index提升10.2%。定量分析进一步表明，VQA推导的语义特征在跨中心散度上显著低于像素级特征，凸显了其在跨中心临床应用中的鲁棒性。

</details>


### [44] [CrossFlow: One-Step Generation Across Latent and Pixel Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19970)
*Xiyuan Wang,Xiao Zhang,Yang Li,Ruoxi Jiang,Zhao Zhong,Liefeng Bo,Muhan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CrossFlow 提出一种跨空间流匹配框架，直接学习从潜变量空间到像素空间的映射，无需单独的解码器。通过免速度的一步目标函数，该方法在 ImageNet-1k 256×256 上以单次函数评估达到 1.62 FID，在保持潜空间效率的同时实现像素级监督。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有潜扩散模型将生成路径置于自编码器潜空间中，但最终样本仍需单独训练的解码器将潜变量映射回像素空间。这种分离导致不匹配：生成器针对潜空间预测进行优化，而最终质量取决于解码器如何处理与干净编码器输出存在差异的生成潜变量。CrossFlow 旨在消除这一分离，直接在训练中实现从潜变量到像素空间的跨空间映射。

Method: CrossFlow 提出一种跨空间流匹配公式，将含噪潜变量输入直接映射为像素空间图像。关键技术是免速度的一步目标函数：潜变量轨迹定义训练路径，但监督预测目标是图像而非潜变量位移。这使得单一模型既能作为一步潜变量到像素的生成器，也能替代潜扩散管线中的解码器。

Result: 在类条件 ImageNet-1k 256×256 数据集上，CrossFlow-XL 以单次函数评估实现 1.62 FID。消融实验表明，潜编码器以及像素级感知损失和对抗损失对保真度至关重要。

Conclusion: 跨空间流目标函数能够在保持潜表示效率的同时结合像素级监督，推理时无需单独的解码器，有效弥合了潜空间生成与像素空间输出之间的鸿沟。

Original Abstract: Most diffusion and flow-matching generators define the prior, probability path, and prediction target in the same representation space. Latent diffusion improves efficiency by moving this path into an autoencoder latent space, but the final sample is still produced by a separately trained decoder. This separation creates a mismatch: the generator is optimized for latent-space prediction, while final quality depends on how the decoder handles generated latents that may differ from clean encoder outputs. We introduce CrossFlow, a cross-space flow formulation that maps noisy latent inputs directly to pixel-space images. The key technical step is a velocity-free one-step objective: the latent trajectory defines the training path, but the supervised prediction is an image rather than a latent displacement. This lets one model act both as a one-step latent-to-pixel generator and as a decoder replacement for latent diffusion pipelines. On class-conditional ImageNet-1k at $256\times256$, CrossFlow-XL achieves 1.62 FID with one function evaluation. Ablations show that the latent encoder and pixel-space perceptual and adversarial losses are important for fidelity. These results indicate that cross-space flow objectives can combine the efficiency of latent representations with direct pixel-space supervision, without requiring a separate decoder at inference.

中文摘要翻译: 大多数扩散模型和流匹配生成器将先验、概率路径和预测目标定义在同一表示空间中。潜扩散模型通过将路径移至自编码器潜空间来提升效率，但最终样本仍由单独训练的解码器生成。这种分离导致不匹配：生成器针对潜空间预测进行优化，而最终质量取决于解码器如何处理与干净编码器输出存在差异的生成潜变量。我们提出 CrossFlow，一种跨空间流公式，将含噪潜变量输入直接映射为像素空间图像。关键技术是免速度的一步目标函数：潜变量轨迹定义训练路径，但监督预测目标是图像而非潜变量位移。这使得单一模型既能作为一步潜变量到像素的生成器，也能替代潜扩散管线中的解码器。在类条件 ImageNet-1k 256×256 数据集上，CrossFlow-XL 以单次函数评估实现 1.62 FID。消融实验表明，潜编码器以及像素级感知损失和对抗损失对保真度至关重要。这些结果表明，跨空间流目标函数能够在保持潜表示效率的同时结合像素级直接监督，推理时无需单独的解码器。

</details>


### [45] [Vision-Reasoning-Guided Occlusion Removal from Light Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.19985)
*Mohamed Youssef,Oliver Bimber*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种视觉-推理引导的光场遮挡去除框架，融合光场积分与视觉语言模型，通过多采样融合策略抑制前景遮挡并恢复细粒度细节，在合成与真实场景中达到最优性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 自然环境中密集前景植被严重遮挡目标，传统方法难以鲁棒恢复场景，亟需结合物理成像约束与语义推理能力实现遮挡鲁棒的场景恢复。

Method: 首先通过光场积分（LFI）融合多视角观测抑制前景遮挡，生成初始可见性增强表示；然后引入视觉语言模型（VLM）作为条件语义先验，根据观测测量指导退化结构恢复与细节重建；最后采用多采样融合策略聚合多个生成假设为统一估计，提升恢复一致性并减少幻觉伪影。

Result: 在四个合成光场基准场景（4-Syn）上取得最高平均SSIM，在结构化与非结构化采集场景中均表现出强泛化能力，达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: 将物理成像约束与视觉语言推理相结合，能有效实现严重遮挡下的鲁棒感知，可应用于搜索救援和探索性机器人导航等任务。

Original Abstract: Occlusion-robust scene recovery remains a major challenge in computational imaging, particularly in natural environments where dense foreground vegetation severely limits visibility. We propose a vision-reasoning-guided light field occlusion removal framework that combines the visibility recovery capability of light field integration (LFI) with the semantic reasoning capacity of vision-language models (VLMs). Multi-view observations are first integrated via LFI to suppress foreground occlusions and produce an initial visibility-enhanced representation. A VLM is then incorporated as a conditional semantic prior to restore degraded structures and recover fine details, guided by the observed measurements. To improve recovery consistency and reduce hallucination artifacts, we introduce a multi-sample fusion strategy that aggregates multiple generated hypotheses into a unified estimate. Experimental results on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate state-of-the-art performance, achieving the highest average SSIM across four synthetic light field benchmark scenes (4-Syn) and strong generalization across structured and unstructured acquisition settings. These results highlight the effectiveness of combining physical imaging constraints with vision-language reasoning for robust perception under severe occlusion, with applicability to search-and-rescue and exploratory robotic navigation.

中文摘要翻译: 遮挡鲁棒的场景恢复在计算成像中仍是一个重大挑战，尤其在自然环境中，密集的前景植被会严重限制可见性。我们提出了一种视觉-推理引导的光场遮挡去除框架，该框架结合了光场积分（LFI）的可见性恢复能力与视觉语言模型（VLM）的语义推理能力。首先通过LFI融合多视角观测以抑制前景遮挡，生成初始可见性增强表示。然后引入VLM作为条件语义先验，根据观测测量指导退化结构的恢复和细粒度细节的重建。为了提升恢复一致性并减少幻觉伪影，我们提出了一种多采样融合策略，将多个生成假设聚合为统一的估计。在合成与真实数据集上的实验结果表明，该方法达到了最先进的性能，在四个合成光场基准场景（4-Syn）上取得了最高的平均结构相似性指数（SSIM），并在结构化与非结构化采集场景中均表现出强泛化能力。这些结果凸显了将物理成像约束与视觉语言推理相结合对于严重遮挡下鲁棒感知的有效性，该方法可应用于搜索救援和探索性机器人导航等场景。

</details>


### [46] [QG-MIL: A Gated Transformer Aggregator for Domain-Agnostic Multiple Instance Learning in Medical Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20027)
*Luca Zedda,Davide Antonio Mura,Cecilia Di Ruberto,Maurizio Atzori,Muhammed Furkan Dasdelen,Carsten Marr,Andrea Loddo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出QG-MIL，一种基于门控Transformer的多实例学习聚合器，通过归一化、QK归一化、输出门控和SwiGLU模块缓解注意力集中问题，在六个医学图像基准上平均提升+6.1 macro F1。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 医学影像中基于注意力的多实例学习聚合器容易出现注意力集中问题，导致预测过于自信且不稳定，亟需一种无需辅助损失或掩码的正则化方法。

Method: QG-MIL采用四个协同架构组件：RMSNorm预归一化、逐头QK归一化、细粒度注意力输出门控、SwiGLU风格前馈模块，共同稳定训练并使注意力分布更均匀。

Result: 在六个涵盖全切片病理和细胞级血液学的基准上，最佳QG-MIL变体全面超越领先基线，平均提升+6.1 macro F1。注意力叠加图和注意力质量分析证实了更均匀的实例权重分布。

Conclusion: QG-MIL设计在跨领域性能上最一致且方差最小，消融实验表明各组件在特定数据集上可匹敌完整模型，但完整设计整体最优。

Original Abstract: Attention-based Multiple Instance Learning aggregators in medical imaging are prone to attention concentration, producing overconfident and unstable predictions. We introduce QG-MIL, a gated transformer aggregator that addresses this through four synergistic architectural components: RMSNorm-based pre-normalization, per-head QK normalization, fine-grained attention output gating, and SwiGLU-style feed-forward modules. Together, these design choices stabilize training and distribute attention more uniformly across instances without auxiliary losses, masking, or multi-stage regularization. We evaluate QG-MIL across six benchmarks spanning whole-slide pathology and cell-level hematology, covering two fundamentally different MIL scales. The best-performing QG-MIL variants outperform leading baselines on all six benchmarks, with an average improvement of +6.1 mean macro F1 points. Attention overlays and attention mass analysis confirm more distributed instance weighting. Ablation studies show that while individual components can match the full model on specific datasets, the QG-MIL design provides the most consistent cross-domain performance and tightest variance when compared to selected baselines. We release a configurable implementation to support reproducibility at: https://github.com/unica-visual-intelligence-lab/QG-MIL

中文摘要翻译: 在医学影像中，基于注意力的多实例学习聚合器容易出现注意力集中问题，从而产生过于自信且不稳定的预测。我们提出QG-MIL，一种门控Transformer聚合器，通过四个协同架构组件解决这一问题：基于RMSNorm的预归一化、逐头QK归一化、细粒度注意力输出门控以及SwiGLU风格的前馈模块。这些设计选择共同稳定了训练过程，并使注意力在实例间分布更加均匀，无需辅助损失、掩码或多阶段正则化。我们在涵盖全切片病理学和细胞级血液学的六个基准上评估了QG-MIL，涉及两种根本不同规模的多实例学习任务。表现最佳的QG-MIL变体在所有六个基准上均优于领先的基线方法，平均提升+6.1个macro F1分数点。注意力叠加图和注意力质量分析证实了更均匀的实例权重分布。消融研究表明，虽然单个组件在特定数据集上可以匹敌完整模型，但QG-MIL设计在与所选基线相比时提供了最一致的跨领域性能和最小的方差。我们发布了可配置的实现以支持可重复性，地址为：https://github.com/unica-visual-intelligence-lab/QG-MIL

</details>


### [47] [ReA-OVCD: Reliability-Aware Open-Vocabulary Change Detection via Semantic and Spatial Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20032)
*Hongming Zhu,Huaji Chen,Bowen Du,Sicong Liu,Qin Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需训练的可靠性感知开放词汇变化检测框架（ReA-OVCD），通过协同语义和空间维度的变化精炼策略，解决现有方法在实例级比较与像素级比较之间的权衡问题，在多个数据集上一致取得最优性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有开放词汇变化检测方法存在固有权衡：实例级比较忽略细粒度语义变化（如建筑局部扩建），而直接像素级比较因语义模糊和空间不一致导致响应不稳定和边界伪影。亟需一种能同时兼顾灵活定位与结果可靠性的方法。

Method: 提出ReA-OVCD框架，包含三个步骤：(1) 从像素级语义差异中导出候选变化区域，确保灵活细粒度定位；(2) 设计语义变化推理模块（SCR），联合分析分布散度和响应变化来重新评估变化，抑制偶然不一致同时保留可靠语义偏移；(3) 设计边界感知变化精炼模块（BCR），通过验证候选区域是否由可靠内部像素支持来缓解边界错位和不确定性导致的伪影。

Result: 在LEVIR-CD、WHU-CD、DSIFN和SECOND等多个数据集上，所提方法一致优于现有最优方法，F1_C指标提升2.13%至9.75%，且计算效率更高。

Conclusion: 通过协同语义推理与边界感知精炼，ReA-OVCD在无需训练的情况下有效解决了开放词汇变化检测中灵活性与可靠性的矛盾，显著提升了检测精度和效率。

Original Abstract: Unlike traditional remote sensing change detection that relies on predefined categories, Open-Vocabulary Change Detection (OVCD) identifies land cover changes flexibly using arbitrary text prompts. However, existing methods suffer from an inherent trade-off when modeling changes: instance-level comparison overlooks fine-grained semantic variations (e.g., partial building extensions), while direct pixel comparison proves unreliable, yielding unstable responses and boundary artifacts due to semantic ambiguity and spatial inconsistency. To this end, we propose an efficient training-free Reliability-Aware Open-Vocabulary Change Detection (ReA-OVCD) framework. It first derives candidate change regions from pixel-wise semantic discrepancies to ensure flexible and detailed localization. To ensure reliability, it subsequently introduces a collaborative refinement strategy to explicitly model change validity from both semantic and spatial perspectives. Specifically, we develop a Semantic Change Reasoning (SCR) module that reassesses changes by jointly analyzing distributional divergence and response variation, enabling the suppression of incidental inconsistencies while preserving reliable semantic shifts. In addition, a Boundary-aware Change Refinement (BCR) module is designed to mitigate artifacts stemming from boundary misalignment and uncertainty through validating whether candidate regions are supported by reliable interior pixels. Extensive experiments across multiple datasets (LEVIR-CD, WHU-CD, DSIFN, and SECOND) demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms state-of-the-art approaches, achieving $\mathrm{F}_{1}^{C}$ improvements of 2.13\% to 9.75\% with higher computational efficiency. The code is publicly available at \https://github.com/Funny0101/ReA-OVCD

中文摘要翻译: 与依赖预定义类别的传统遥感变化检测不同，开放词汇变化检测（OVCD）能够使用任意文本提示灵活识别土地覆盖变化。然而，现有方法在建模变化时存在固有权衡：实例级比较忽视细粒度语义变化（如建筑局部扩建），而直接像素级比较因语义模糊和空间不一致导致响应不稳定和边界伪影，被证明不可靠。为此，我们提出了一种高效且无需训练的可靠性感知开放词汇变化检测（ReA-OVCD）框架。该框架首先从像素级语义差异中导出候选变化区域，以确保灵活且精细的定位。为确保可靠性，随后引入协同精炼策略，从语义和空间两个维度显式建模变化有效性。具体而言，我们开发了语义变化推理（SCR）模块，通过联合分析分布散度和响应变化来重新评估变化，从而抑制偶然不一致性同时保留可靠的语义偏移。此外，设计了边界感知变化精炼（BCR）模块，通过验证候选区域是否由可靠内部像素支持来缓解边界错位和不确定性导致的伪影。在多个数据集（LEVIR-CD、WHU-CD、DSIFN和SECOND）上的广泛实验表明，我们的方法在计算效率更高的同时，一致优于现有最优方法，F1_C指标提升2.13%至9.75%。代码已公开于\https://github.com/Funny0101/ReA-OVCD。

</details>


### [48] [PU-UNet: Stable Multiplicative Interactions for Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20035)
*Ziyuan Li,Osamah Sufyan,Uwe Jaekel,Babette Dellen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PU-UNet，一种将稳定的乘积单元残差块集成到U-Net低分辨率阶段的网络，用于医学图像分割。该方法通过平滑正映射和对数域裁剪实现稳定的乘法特征学习，在多个数据集上取得优于基线Residual U-Net的分割性能，且参数和计算量几乎不变。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有密集预测网络多依赖加法特征变换，对高阶特征交互的建模仅为隐式。乘积单元虽能提供显式的乘法特征建模，但其对数-指数形式存在数值不稳定性，限制了其在深度密集预测网络中的应用。

Method: 提出PU-UNet，在U-Net的低分辨率阶段集成稳定的乘积单元残差块。通过平滑正映射和对数域裁剪实现稳定的乘法特征学习，仅引入可忽略的计算开销。

Result: 在ISIC 2018、Kvasir-SEG和BUSI数据集上Dice分数分别达0.942、0.959和0.925；相比匹配的Residual U-Net基线，Dice和IoU一致提升，参数、FLOPs和推理延迟几乎不变；正常BUSI样本的图像级假阳性率从0.077降至0。

Conclusion: 稳定的乘积单元残差学习是增强U-Net风格分割网络显式乘法交互的有效方式。

Original Abstract: Many dense prediction networks rely on additive feature transformations and model higher-order feature interactions only implicitly. Product units provide an explicit mechanism for multiplicative feature modeling, but their logarithmic--exponential formulation can cause numerical instability, which has limited their use in deep dense prediction networks. In this work, we propose Product-Unit U-Net (PU-UNet), a residual U-Net that integrates stable product-unit residual blocks into rich low-resolution stages for medical image segmentation. The proposed formulation combines smooth positivity mapping with log-domain clipping, enabling stable multiplicative feature learning with negligible computational overhead. On ISIC 2018, Kvasir-SEG, and BUSI, PU-UNet achieves Dice scores of 0.942, 0.959, and up to 0.925, respectively. Compared with a matched Residual U-Net baseline, PU-UNet consistently improves Dice and IoU while keeping parameters, FLOPs, and inference latency nearly unchanged, and reduces the image-level false-positive rate on normal BUSI cases from 0.077 to zero. Ablation studies suggest that the gains are associated with product-unit interactions, are strongest under low-resolution placement, and benefit from the proposed stabilization design. These results suggest that stable product-unit residual learning can be an effective way to enhance U-Net-style segmentation networks with explicit multiplicative interactions.

中文摘要翻译: 许多密集预测网络依赖加性特征变换，仅以隐式方式建模高阶特征交互。乘积单元为乘法特征建模提供了显式机制，但其对数-指数形式可能导致数值不稳定性，这限制了它们在深度密集预测网络中的应用。本文提出乘积单元U-Net（PU-UNet），一种将稳定的乘积单元残差块集成到丰富低分辨率阶段的残差U-Net，用于医学图像分割。所提出的公式结合了平滑正映射与对数域裁剪，从而在可忽略的计算开销下实现稳定的乘法特征学习。在ISIC 2018、Kvasir-SEG和BUSI数据集上，PU-UNet的Dice分数分别达到0.942、0.959和高达0.925。与匹配的Residual U-Net基线相比，PU-UNet在保持参数、FLOPs和推理延迟几乎不变的同时，持续提升了Dice和IoU指标，并将正常BUSI病例的图像级假阳性率从0.077降至零。消融研究表明，性能提升与乘积单元交互相关，在低分辨率放置下效果最强，且得益于所提出的稳定化设计。这些结果表明，稳定的乘积单元残差学习可以成为通过显式乘法交互增强U-Net风格分割网络的有效途径。

</details>


### [49] [FUSE: Frequency-domain Unification and Spectral Energy Alignment for Multi-modal Object Re-Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20044)
*Xuanhao Qi,Tom H. Luan,Yukang Zhang,Jinkai Zheng,Zhou Su,Shuwei Li,Lei Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FUSE是一个频域多模态行人重识别框架，通过频谱解缠与能量对齐，在三个基准数据集上取得9%以上的性能提升。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有多模态ReID方法过度依赖低频线索（颜色、光照、粗略外观），忽略了中高频结构（几何、纹理等身份判别细节），导致频谱表示不完整和跨模态对齐不稳定。

Method: 提出频域框架FUSE，包含频谱解缠模块（SDM）自适应地将特征分解为低、中、高三个频率子空间进行层次化建模，以及跨模态对齐模块（CAM）通过频率一致性正则化实现能量对齐和子空间互补性约束，同时引入可学习频率调制增强对光照变化和异构传感器的鲁棒性。

Result: 在RGBNT201、RGBNT100和MSVR310三个数据集上，mAP提升9.1%，Rank-1提升9.5%。

Conclusion: FUSE建立了一种可解释的频域多模态表示学习范式，有效弥补了现有方法在高频信息建模上的不足。

Original Abstract: Despite significant progress in multi-modal Re-Identification (ReID), existing methods tend to emphasize low-frequency cues. Consequently, they focus on attributes such as color, illumination, and coarse appearance, while overlooking mid and high-frequency structures that encode geometric, textural, and identity-discriminative details. This imbalance leads to incomplete spectral representations and unstable cross-modal alignment. To overcome these limitations, we introduce FUSE, a frequency-domain framework that reformulates multi-modal ReID as a two-stage process of spectral disentanglement and energy alignment. The proposed Spectral Decomposition Module (SDM) adaptively partitions features into low, mid, and high-frequency subspaces, enabling hierarchical spectral modeling. The Cross-Modal Alignment Module (CAM) further enforces energy alignment and subspace complementarity across modalities via frequency-consistency regularization. In addition, FUSE incorporates learnable frequency modulation to enhance robustness under varying illumination and heterogeneous sensor conditions. Extensive experiments on RGBNT201, RGBNT100, and MSVR310 show that FUSE achieves 9.1\% mAP and 9.5\% Rank-1 improvements, establishing an interpretable frequency-domain paradigm for multi-modal representation learning.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管多模态行人重识别（ReID）已取得显著进展，但现有方法倾向于强调低频线索。因此，它们关注颜色、光照和粗略外观等属性，而忽略了编码几何、纹理和身份判别细节的中频与高频结构。这种不平衡导致了不完整的频谱表示和不稳定的跨模态对齐。为了克服这些局限性，我们提出了FUSE，一个频域框架，将多模态ReID重构为频谱解缠与能量对齐的两阶段过程。所提出的频谱解缠模块（SDM）自适应地将特征划分为低、中、高三个频率子空间，实现层次化频谱建模。跨模态对齐模块（CAM）通过频率一致性正则化，进一步强制实现跨模态的能量对齐和子空间互补性。此外，FUSE引入了可学习的频率调制，以增强在变化光照和异构传感器条件下的鲁棒性。在RGBNT201、RGBNT100和MSVR310上的大量实验表明，FUSE取得了9.1%的mAP和9.5%的Rank-1提升，为多模态表示学习建立了一种可解释的频域范式。

</details>


### [50] [See-and-Reach: Precise Vision-Language Navigation for UAVs within the Field of View](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20045)
*Fanfu Xue,En Yu,Yantian Shen,Zhikun Hu,Hongjun Wang,Yang Yang,Xindi Wang,Jiande Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对UAV视觉语言导航（UAV-VLN）中目标可见但难以精确到达的问题，提出了目标可见导航任务UAV-VLN-FOV，并设计了动态3D方向引导的视觉语言航点预测框架3DG-VLN，通过自适应处理高分辨率前视和下视观测、在线更新目标相对方向，显著提升了终端到达能力，在基准测试和真实实验中均取得优异效果。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有UAV-VLN通常被建模为整体搜索与到达问题，将远程目标发现和最终目标接近联合优化与评估，难以单独衡量飞行器在目标进入视野后准确接地可见目标并将视觉语言证据转化为精确3D运动的关键能力。

Method: 提出3DG-VLN框架：1）自适应处理高分辨率前视和下视观测，保留细粒度视觉与几何细节用于目标接地；2）在闭环导航过程中在线更新目标相对方向，保持空间对齐并减少累积方向漂移；3）构建包含2,717条轨迹的高分辨率基准，带有目标导向的高层指令、高分辨率前视/下视自中心观测和连续3D航点标注。

Result: 3DG-VLN在多个竞争性UAV-VLN基线上表现最优，成功率提升13.82%；真实世界实验进一步验证了其在实际"看到-到达"导航任务中的潜力。

Conclusion: 通过将目标可见导航任务从整体UAV-VLN中分离出来，并引入动态3D方向引导的航点预测方法，可以有效提升飞行器在目标视野内精确到达的能力，为实际应用中的精确视觉导航提供了可行方案。

Original Abstract: UAV Vision-Language Navigation (UAV-VLN) is typically formulated as a holistic search-and-reach problem, where long-range target discovery and final target approach are optimized and evaluated jointly. This formulation makes it difficult to assess a critical capability of aerial embodied agents, namely whether a UAV can accurately ground a visible target and translate vision-language evidence into precise 3D motion once the target enters its field of view. To address this limitation, we introduce UAV-VLN-FOV, a target-visible navigation task that isolates the see-and-reach stage and enables a more diagnostic evaluation of terminal reaching ability. We further propose 3DG-VLN, a vision-language waypoint prediction framework guided by dynamic 3D direction cues to enhance fine-grained visual grounding and spatial direction alignment for precise target reaching. Specifically, 3DG-VLN adaptively processes high-resolution front-view and downward-view observations to preserve fine-grained visual and geometric details for target grounding. It also updates the target-relative direction online during closed-loop navigation, allowing the agent to maintain spatial alignment with the target and reduce accumulated direction drift. To support this task, we construct a dedicated high-resolution benchmark which contains 2,717 trajectories with target-oriented high-level instructions, high-resolution front-view and downward-view egocentric observations, and continuous 3D waypoint annotations. Experiments show that 3DG-VLN outperforms competitive UAV-VLN baselines, achieving a 13.82\% improvement in success rate. Real-world trials further demonstrate the potential of 3DG-VLN for practical see-and-reach navigation. The source code and benchmark are available at https://github.com/xuefanfu/3DG-VLN.

中文摘要翻译: 无人机视觉语言导航（UAV-VLN）通常被形式化为一个整体性的搜索与到达问题，其中远程目标发现和最终目标接近被联合优化与评估。这种提法使得评估空中具身智能体的一个关键能力变得困难，即当目标进入飞行器视野后，无人机能否准确地接地可见目标，并将视觉语言证据转化为精确的3D运动。为了解决这一局限，我们引入了UAV-VLN-FOV，这是一个目标可见的导航任务，它分离出"看到-到达"阶段，从而能够对终端到达能力进行更具诊断性的评估。我们进一步提出了3DG-VLN，这是一个由动态3D方向线索引导的视觉语言航点预测框架，旨在增强细粒度视觉接地和空间方向对齐，以实现精确的目标到达。具体而言，3DG-VLN自适应地处理高分辨率前视和下视观测，保留细粒度的视觉和几何细节用于目标接地。它还在闭环导航过程中在线更新目标相对方向，使智能体能够保持与目标的空间对齐并减少累积的方向漂移。为了支持该任务，我们构建了一个专用的高分辨率基准，包含2,717条具有目标导向高层指令的轨迹、高分辨率前视和下视自中心观测，以及连续的3D航点标注。实验表明，3DG-VLN优于竞争性的UAV-VLN基线方法，成功率提升了13.82%。真实世界实验进一步展示了3DG-VLN在实际"看到-到达"导航任务中的潜力。源代码和基准数据可在 https://github.com/xuefanfu/3DG-VLN 获取。

</details>


### [51] [Variable-Length Tokenization via Learnable Global Merging for Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20076)
*Dong Hoon Lee,Seunghoon Hong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于令牌合并的变长分词器，用于扩散模型，通过可学习的全局合并实现跨长度表示对齐，从而改善质量-计算量权衡。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统变长分词器通过截断有序令牌序列来调节长度，导致令牌语义依赖于位置，造成跨长度表示不对齐，使得单个变长扩散模型难以有效运行。

Method: 提出基于合并的变长分词器：鼓励相似令牌合并以实现跨长度表示对齐；并引入可学习全局合并（数据无关），使合并模式在生成阶段可用，从而兼容扩散Transformer。

Result: 在ImageNet 256×256生成任务上，所提方法在gFID-计算量权衡上优于先前变长分词器方法。

Conclusion: 基于合并的变长分词器能有效解决变长扩散模型中的跨长度分布偏移问题，实现更优的质量-计算权衡。

Original Abstract: Latent Diffusion Models (LDMs) have become dominant in visual synthesis, but their quality-compute trade-off is largely constrained by the tokenizer's fixed compression ratio. Variable-length tokenizers (VLTs) promise adaptive compression by varying token counts, allowing diffusion models to flexibly balance quality and compute. However, conventional VLTs modulate length by truncating ordered token sequences, which makes token semantics depend on token position and breaks representational alignment across lengths. This leads to a cross-length shift in the latent distribution that hinders a single variable-length diffusion model from operating effectively. To address this, we propose a novel variable-length tokenizer that modulates length by merging tokens. We show that encouraging similar tokens to merge enables direct cross-length representation alignment when the diffusion transformer operates according to the merging pattern. Since conventional merging methods are data-dependent, making the merging pattern inaccessible during generation, we introduce learnable global merging, which is data-independent, to ensure compatibility with diffusion transformers. On ImageNet 256$\times$256 generation, our merging-based variable-length tokenizer integrated with a diffusion transformer achieves a superior gFID-compute trade-off compared to prior VLT methods. Code is available at [this https URL](https://github.com/movinghoon/lgm)

中文摘要翻译: 潜在扩散模型（LDMs）已成为视觉合成领域的主导方法，但其质量与计算量之间的权衡在很大程度上受限于分词器的固定压缩比。变长分词器（VLTs）通过改变令牌数量实现自适应压缩，使扩散模型能够灵活地平衡质量与计算量。然而，传统VLT通过截断有序令牌序列来调节长度，这使得令牌语义依赖于令牌位置，破坏了不同长度之间的表示对齐。这导致潜在分布出现跨长度偏移，阻碍了单个变长扩散模型的有效运行。为解决这一问题，我们提出一种新颖的基于令牌合并的变长分词器。我们表明，当扩散Transformer按照合并模式运行时，鼓励相似令牌合并能够实现直接的跨长度表示对齐。由于传统合并方法是数据依赖的，导致生成过程中无法获得合并模式，我们引入了可学习的全局合并方法，该方法数据无关，确保与扩散Transformer的兼容性。在ImageNet 256×256生成任务上，我们将基于合并的变长分词器与扩散Transformer集成，相较于先前的VLT方法，实现了更优的gFID-计算量权衡。代码已开源。

</details>


### [52] [The Hidden Evolution of Disguised Visual Context inside the VLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20077)
*Wish Suharitdamrong,Tony Alex,Muhammad Awais,Sara Atito*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了视觉-语言模型中两种视觉令牌集成范式（上下文注入与逐层注入），揭示了视觉令牌在LLM内部从原始视觉信号逐步演化为语言对齐表示的动态过程，并发现不同范式捕捉了视觉信号的不同频率特征，最终影响模型在不同任务上的表现。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当前视觉-语言模型（VLM）中视觉令牌的集成架构（上下文提示注入 vs. 逐层注入）对视觉信息在LLM内部的转化过程及与语言空间交互的影响尚缺乏系统的对比和理解。

Method: 在完全相同的训练条件下，对上下文注入和逐层注入两种VLM集成范式进行公平比较，在单图、多图和视频基准上评估，并通过分析视觉令牌在LLM各层的演化过程来揭示其频率特征和表示质量。

Result: 视觉令牌进入LLM时表现为"伪装的视觉上下文"——缺乏语言结构的原始表征，但会根据集成范式逐步重塑；不同范式捕捉视觉信号的不同频率特征；注意力分配本身不足以解释性能，各层的视觉表示质量才是性能驱动因素。

Conclusion: VLM的集成范式决定了视觉令牌在LLM内部的演化路径，影响了模型能有效利用的视觉特征、视觉表示与语言空间的对齐程度，以及不同任务上的表现。

Original Abstract: Visual tokens enter Large Language Models (LLMs) as raw, foreign signals. How they are transformed into meaningful representations and interact with the language space depends entirely on the integration architecture. Whether by treating visual tokens as in-context prompts within the input sequence or injecting them directly into the LLM's intermediate layers. A controlled comparison and understanding of how these architectural choices affect visual information and its internal transformation to integrate with the LLM remains underexplored. We provide a fair comparison by evaluating in-context and layer-wise injection VLM integration paradigms under identical training conditions across single image, multi-image, and video benchmarks. In doing so, we uncover a hidden evolution where visual tokens enter the LLM as disguised visual context, raw representations lacking linguistic structure, but are progressively reshaped depending on the integration paradigm, each capturing fundamentally different frequency characteristics of the visual signal. We show that this evolution inside the LLM determines what visual features the VLM can utilize effectively, how visual representations align with the language space, and ultimately how each paradigm performs across different tasks. We further demonstrate that attention allocation alone is insufficient, and that performance is driven by the quality of visual representations at each layer.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉令牌作为原始的外部信号进入大语言模型（LLM）。它们如何被转化为有意义的表示并与语言空间交互，完全取决于集成架构——无论是将视觉令牌视为输入序列中的上下文提示，还是直接将其注入LLM的中间层。目前，关于这些架构选择如何影响视觉信息及其在LLM内部转化以融入语言空间的过程，尚缺乏受控对比和深入理解。我们在完全相同的训练条件下，对上下文注入和逐层注入两种视觉-语言模型（VLM）集成范式进行了公平比较，在单图、多图和视频基准上进行了评估。通过这一研究，我们揭示了一个隐藏的演化过程：视觉令牌进入LLM时表现为伪装的视觉上下文——即缺乏语言结构的原始表征——但会根据集成范式逐步重塑，每种范式捕捉了视觉信号中根本不同的频率特征。我们表明，LLM内部的这一演化过程决定了VLM能够有效利用哪些视觉特征、视觉表示如何与语言空间对齐，以及最终每种范式在不同任务上的表现。我们进一步证明，仅凭注意力分配是不够的，性能实际上由每一层的视觉表示质量驱动。

</details>


### [53] [Holo-World: Unified Camera, Object and Weather Control for Video World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20083)
*Xiangchen Yin,Wenzhang Sun,Jiahui Yuan,Zijie Liu,Yinda Chen,Wei Li,Dachun Kai,Chunfeng Wang,Xiaoyan Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Holo-World，一个基于单帧图像、支持相机/物体/天气联合可控的视频世界模型，通过分解式场景适配器和解耦引导策略，实现保持场景结构的同时生成目标天气状态的视频。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视频世界模型对相机、物体和天气的控制相互孤立，且天气生成通常依赖已有视频或重建场景，缺乏从单张图像出发、同时控制场景状态（如天气）的能力。

Method: 构建HoloStateData数据集提供统一控制样本；提出Holo-World模型，其核心是Unified Scene Adapter将世界保持与天气迁移分解为不同参数子空间，结合渲染背景、几何缓冲和物体控制来维持场景结构并建模天气外观；采用Scene-Weather Decomposed CFG分别引导场景和天气残差以增强目标天气效果。

Result: 定量和定性实验表明，Holo-World在精确控制相机和物体运动、保持场景结构一致性的同时，能将场景迁移到多种目标天气状态，在天气状态生成任务上优于视频到视频的天气编辑基线方法。

Conclusion: Holo-World实现了从单帧图像出发、联合控制相机/物体/天气的统一可控视频世界模型，在场景保持和天气迁移方面均取得优异效果。

Original Abstract: Video world models are moving toward preserving an observed world under controllable camera and object motion while allowing its environmental state to change. Yet these controls remain isolated, and weather generation typically relies on a source video or reconstructed scene that already specifies future structure. We study a first-frame-anchored source-to-state setting, where the model starts from a single image and follows explicit camera and object controls and an optional weather instruction, then generates a video that either preserves the source world or transfers it to a target weather state. To address these challenges, we first build HoloStateData, a state video dataset that turns diverse videos into unified control samples for camera, object, and weather supervision. Second, we introduce Holo-World, a unified controllable video world model that jointly controls scene from a single image. Its Unified Scene Adapter factorizes world preservation and weather transfer into distinct parameter subspaces, using rendered background, geometry buffers, and object controls to maintain controlled scene structure while modeling weather-dependent appearance and particle effects. Additionally, Scene-Weather Decomposed CFG guides scene and weather residuals separately, strengthening target weather effects without over-amplifying the full condition. Quantitative and qualitative experiments demonstrate that Holo-World maintains precise camera and object control with consistent scene structure while transferring scenes into diverse target weather state, outperforming video-to-video weather editing baselines on weather-state generation. Our project page is available at \url{https://xiangchenyin.github.io/Holo-World/}.

中文摘要翻译: 视频世界模型正朝着在可控相机和物体运动下保持观察到的世界、同时允许其环境状态变化的方向发展。然而这些控制仍然相互孤立，且天气生成通常依赖于已经指定了未来结构的源视频或重建场景。我们研究了一种以第一帧为锚点的源到状态设置，模型从单张图像出发，遵循明确的相机和物体控制以及可选的天气指令，然后生成一个要么保持源世界、要么将其迁移到目标天气状态的视频。为了解决这些挑战，我们首先构建了HoloStateData，这是一个状态视频数据集，将多样化的视频转换为用于相机、物体和天气监督的统一控制样本。其次，我们引入了Holo-World，一个统一的、可从单张图像联合控制场景的可控视频世界模型。其统一场景适配器将世界保持和天气迁移分解为不同的参数子空间，利用渲染背景、几何缓冲和物体控制来维持受控的场景结构，同时建模依赖天气的外观和粒子效果。此外，场景-天气解耦CFG分别引导场景和天气残差，在不过度放大完整条件的情况下增强目标天气效果。定量和定性实验表明，Holo-World在保持一致场景结构的同时实现了精确的相机和物体控制，并能将场景迁移到多种目标天气状态，在天气状态生成任务上优于视频到视频的天气编辑基线方法。我们的项目页面可在\url{https://xiangchenyin.github.io/Holo-World/}获取。

</details>


### [54] [EventVLA: Event-Driven Visual Evidence Memory for Long-Horizon Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20092)
*Ganlin Yang,Zhangzheng Tu,Yuqiang Yang,Sitong Mao,Junyi Dong,Tianxing Chen,Jiaqi Peng,Jing Xiong,Jiafei Cao,Jifeng Dai,Wengang Zhou,Yao Mu,Tai Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EventVLA提出了一种基于稀疏视觉证据记忆的端到端框架，通过动态关键帧证据记忆（KEM）模块预测未来关键帧概率，在长时程机器人操作任务中显著优于现有方法，平均成功率提升40%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有记忆增强型VLA策略存在三大问题：信息瓶颈严重、解耦双系统延迟高、无选择性缓冲累积大量视觉冗余，导致长时程操作中任务相关线索被遮挡或不可观测时策略失效。

Method: EventVLA包含两个核心组件：1）基础视觉锚点（Foundational Visual Anchors），用于保留初始和短期上下文；2）动态关键帧证据记忆（KEM）模块，直接从VLA的隐层嵌入预测未来关键帧概率，自主捕获并存储稀疏的任务关键视觉事件，实现前瞻驱动的动态记忆管理。

Result: 在17个需要记忆的仿真任务和4个真实世界双臂操作任务上，EventVLA相较于最先进的记忆增强型VLA平均成功率提升40%。此外，还提出了用于评估非马尔可夫操作任务的诊断基准RoboTwin-MeM。

Conclusion: EventVLA通过稀疏视觉证据记忆和前瞻性关键帧选择机制，有效解决了长时程机器人操作中的记忆瓶颈问题，验证了动态、选择性记忆对提升VLA策略鲁棒性的重要价值。

Original Abstract: Memory remains a critical bottleneck for long-horizon robotic manipulation, as standard Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies often fail when task-relevant cues become occluded or unobservable over time. While existing memory-augmented methods utilize historical context, they either suffer from severe information bottlenecks, incur high latency via decoupled dual systems, or rely on unselective buffers that accumulate massive visual redundancies. To address these limitations, we introduce EventVLA, an end-to-end framework founded on the concept of sparse visual evidence memory that comprises two core components: foundational visual anchors to retain initial and short-term contexts, and a dynamic Keyframe Evidence Memory (KEM) module. Specifically, KEM directly predicts future keyframe probabilities from the VLA's latent embeddings to autonomously capture and store sparse, task-critical visual events. This foresight-driven mechanism empowers the policy to dynamically evaluate the future causal utility of current observations, preserving transient visual evidence before it becomes unobservable. Furthermore, we propose RoboTwin-MeM, a diagnostic benchmark specifically designed to evaluate non-Markovian manipulation tasks with interactive visual evidence. Extensive evaluations show that across 17 memory-requiring simulation tasks and 4 real-world bimanual tasks, EventVLA achieves an average success rate improvement of +40% over state-of-the-art memory-augmented VLAs.

中文摘要翻译: 内存仍然是长时程机器人操作的关键瓶颈，因为标准的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略在任务相关线索随时间被遮挡或变得不可观测时往往失败。尽管现有的记忆增强方法利用历史上下文，但它们要么遭受严重的信息瓶颈，要么通过解耦的双系统引入高延迟，要么依赖无选择性缓冲区而累积大量视觉冗余。为了解决这些局限性，我们引入EventVLA，一个基于稀疏视觉证据记忆概念的端到端框架，包含两个核心组件：用于保留初始和短期上下文的基础视觉锚点，以及动态关键帧证据记忆（KEM）模块。具体而言，KEM直接从VLA的隐层嵌入预测未来关键帧概率，以自主捕获并存储稀疏的任务关键视觉事件。这种前瞻驱动的机制使策略能够动态评估当前观测的未来因果效用，在瞬态视觉证据变得不可观测之前将其保留。此外，我们提出了RoboTwin-MeM，一个专门设计用于评估具有交互式视觉证据的非马尔可夫操作任务的诊断基准。大量实验表明，在17个需要记忆的仿真任务和4个真实世界双臂操作任务中，EventVLA相较于最先进的记忆增强型VLA实现了平均40%的成功率提升。

</details>


### [55] [MakeupMirror: Improving Facial Attribute Preservation in Diffusion Models for Makeup Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20094)
*Nefeli Andreou,Angel Martínez-González,Sabine Sternig,Matthieu Guillaumin,Epameinondas Antonakos,Michael Opitz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MakeupMirror，一种基于扩散模型的化妆迁移方法，通过面部几何条件控制、区域特定化妆迁移、肤色调制和快速采样等技术，在保持面部身份特征和肤色的前提下实现高保真化妆迁移，相较Stable-Makeup在面部识别相似度上提升60%，肤色差异降低50%，推理延迟仅0.7秒。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型的化妆迁移方法（如Stable-Makeup）虽然提升了准确性和真实性，但在身份特征保持和肤色保留方面仍存在不足，无法满足生产级虚拟试妆需求。本文旨在解决这些问题，实现更逼真、更保真的化妆迁移。

Method: (1) 使用ControlNets集成面部几何条件约束以保持面部保真度；(2) 区域特定的化妆迁移控制，实现皮肤、眼睛、嘴唇等不同面部区域的精准化妆应用；(3) 基于肤色的化妆迁移调制，防止跨主体迁移时肤色改变；(4) 集成Levenberg-Marquardt Langevin采样器，在保持生成质量的同时加速推理。

Result: 在CPM-Real、Makeup Wild和新收集的MakeupSelfies数据集上，相比Stable-Makeup：面部识别相似度提升60%，肤色差异降低50%，推理延迟为0.7秒，核心面部身份保持标准下专家接受率达94%。

Conclusion: MakeupMirror在面部特征保持和肤色保留方面取得了显著进展，实现了生产级虚拟试妆的可行性，为在线化妆购物和增强现实体验提供了更可靠的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Makeup transfer models enable fun augmented reality (AR) experiences as well as virtual try-on (VTO) for online makeup shopping. While recent state-of-the-art diffusion based solutions such as Stable-Makeup dramatically improve the accuracy and realism of makeup transfer, they still face limitations in identity and skin color preservation, making production-level VTO for makeup shopping unrealistic. In this work, we propose MakeupMirror, a diffusion-based approach to makeup transfer that makes significant progress towards preserving facial features and skin tone. We introduce several technical innovations over Stable-Makeup: (1) integration of facial geometry conditioning with ControlNets to maintain facial fidelity; (2) region-specific makeup transfer control to enable precise makeup application across facial regions such as skin, eyes and lips; (3) skin tone-based makeup transfer modulation that prevent skin tone alteration in cross-subject transfer scenarios; and (4) integration of a Levenberg-Marquardt Langevin sampler to speed up inference while maintaining generation quality. Our experiments on CPM-Real, Makeup Wild, and (herein newly collected, more diverse) MakeupSelfies datasets show that MakeupMirror improves relative facial recognition similarity by +60%, reduces relative skin tone difference by -50% over Stable-Makeup, with a latency of 0.7s, while achieving expert acceptance rate of 94% across core facial identity preservation criteria.

中文摘要翻译: 化妆迁移模型能够实现有趣的增强现实（AR）体验以及在线化妆购物的虚拟试妆（VTO）。尽管近期最先进的基于扩散模型的解决方案（如Stable-Makeup）显著提升了化妆迁移的准确性和真实性，但在身份保持和肤色保留方面仍存在局限，使得面向生产级的化妆购物虚拟试妆难以落地。本文提出MakeupMirror，一种基于扩散模型的化妆迁移方法，在保留面部特征和肤色方面取得了显著进展。我们在Stable-Makeup基础上引入了多项技术创新：(1) 集成ControlNets的面部几何条件约束以保持面部保真度；(2) 区域特定的化妆迁移控制，实现皮肤、眼睛和嘴唇等面部区域的精准化妆应用；(3) 基于肤色的化妆迁移调制，防止跨主体迁移场景中的肤色改变；(4) 集成Levenberg-Marquardt Langevin采样器，在保持生成质量的同时加速推理。我们在CPM-Real、Makeup Wild以及本文新收集的更具多样性的MakeupSelfies数据集上的实验表明，相比Stable-Makeup，MakeupMirror将面部识别相对相似度提升了60%，相对肤色差异降低了50%，推理延迟为0.7秒，同时核心面部身份保持标准的专家接受率达到94%。

</details>


### [56] [Stitching and dimensionality effects on large artificially generated volume datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20095)
*Lucas von Chamier,Jan Philipp Albrecht,Dagmar Kainmüller*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究分块拼接伪影对生成式风格迁移模型的影响，发现FID无法检测影响下游分割的细微伪影，2D与3D模型各有优劣，且感知指标不足以评估生物医学图像中的域适应质量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 尽管拼接伪影已知会影响分割任务，但其对用于风格迁移的生成模型的影响尚不清楚，且现有感知质量指标（如FID）能否有效检测这类伪影也未得到验证。

Method: 使用基于cycleGAN的模型，在冷冻电镜数据集上比较三种拼接策略和两种分块维度（2D vs 3D），从感知质量和下游线粒体分割性能两个维度评估拼接伪影的影响。

Result: (1) FID分数无法检测出对下游分割性能有显著影响的细微拼接伪影；(2) 无伪影拼接的3D模型在下游任务上略优于2D模型，但提升幅度不足以证明其计算开销的合理性；(3) 2D模型因批次大小更大而训练更稳定；(4) 三个正交方向的集成预测可提升低质量数据，但对高质量输出无帮助。

Conclusion: 在大型科学数据集上最大化生成模型性能需要仔细考虑并缓解拼接伪影，且仅靠感知指标不足以评估生物医学图像中的域适应质量。

Original Abstract: Generating large images via deep learning requires patching input data to accommodate hardware memory limitations, then assembling output patches, a process that can introduce stitching artifacts when neighboring patches do not align at borders. While these artifacts are known to affect segmentation tasks, their impact on generative models for style-transfer remains poorly understood. We investigated three stitching approaches and two patch dimensionalities (2D vs 3D) using cycleGAN models trained on cryo-electron microscopy datasets. We evaluated both perceptual quality and performance on downstream mitochondria segmentation. Our key findings reveal that: (1) FID scores fail to detect subtle stitching artifacts that significantly impact downstream segmentation performance, (2) 3D models with artifact-free stitching marginally outperform 2D models on downstream tasks, though the improvement barely justifies the computational cost, and (3) 2D models train more stably due to larger batch sizes. Additionally, we demonstrate that ensembling predictions from three orthogonal directions can improve low-quality volumes but provides no benefit for high-quality outputs. These results demonstrate that maximizing generative model performance on large scientific datasets requires careful consideration and mitigation of stitching artifacts, and that perceptual metrics alone are insufficient for evaluating domain adaptation quality in biomedical imaging.

中文摘要翻译: 通过深度学习生成大尺寸图像时，需要对输入数据进行分块处理以适应硬件内存限制，再对输出分块进行拼接。当相邻分块在边界处无法对齐时，这一过程可能会引入拼接伪影。虽然已知这些伪影会影响分割任务，但它们对用于风格迁移的生成模型的影响尚不清楚。我们使用在冷冻电镜数据集上训练的cycleGAN模型，研究了三种拼接策略和两种分块维度（2D vs 3D）。我们从感知质量和下游线粒体分割性能两个角度进行了评估。关键发现如下：(1) FID分数无法检测出对下游分割性能有显著影响的细微拼接伪影；(2) 无伪影拼接的3D模型在下游任务上略优于2D模型，但性能提升几乎不足以证明其计算开销的合理性；(3) 2D模型由于批次大小更大，训练更为稳定。此外，我们证明了从三个正交方向集成预测可以提升低质量数据，但对高质量输出无益。这些结果表明，在大型科学数据集上最大化生成模型性能需要仔细考虑并缓解拼接伪影，且仅靠感知指标不足以评估生物医学图像中的域适应质量。

</details>


### [57] [WeGenBench: A Multidimensional Diagnostic Benchmark towards Text-to-Image Model Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20100)
*Qian Liang,Xiaomin Li,Ying Zhang,Jia Xu,Lihao Ni,Hongrui Li,Jingjing Li,Jing Lyu,Chen Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WeGenBench是一个用于全面、多角度评估文生图模型能力的新基准，包含4000个中英双语测试提示，通过场景分类与多维标签的交叉评估机制，结合基于VLM的新指标，精准定位模型缺陷。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基准难以全面准确地从多个维度衡量文生图模型的性能，无法揭示模型在特定类别中的固有缺陷。

Method: 构建包含4000个中英双语测试提示的基准，采用场景分类与多维标签相结合的交叉评估机制，并设计基于视觉-语言模型（VLM）的新评估指标，从三个核心方面评估模型性能，同时输出评估结果与详细推理轨迹。

Result: 对当前最先进方法进行了系统基准测试，深入分析了现有模型的局限性。

Conclusion: WeGenBench能够精准定位模型在特定生成类别中的不足，并提供了可验证的评估推理过程。

Original Abstract: Recent text-to-image generation models have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in synthesizing highly realistic images from text inputs alone. Although existing benchmarks can evaluate the generation capabilities of various models to some extent, they struggle to comprehensively and accurately measure performance across multiple dimensions, often failing to reveal the inherent deficiencies of models in specific categories. To address these limitations, we propose WeGenBench, a novel benchmark designed for the comprehensive, multi-perspective evaluation of text-to-image generation capabilities. Our benchmark comprises a total of 4,000 test prompts across two primary categories, meticulously balanced between Chinese and English to evaluate bilingual and cross-cultural generation capabilities. Beyond macroscopic scene classification, we annotate each prompt with multi-dimensional tags tailored to the distinct content and challenges of each language, thereby refining the generation tasks into more specific sub-categories. Through a cross-dimensional evaluation mechanism leveraging both scene classifications and multi-dimensional tags, WeGenBench can precisely pinpoint model shortcomings in specific generation categories. Furthermore, to measure generation quality more accurately, we design and validate several novel evaluation metrics by integrating Vision-Language Models (VLMs), which assess model performance on domain-specific tasks from three core aspects. Crucially, our approach yields both the assessment outcomes and the detailed reasoning trajectories, facilitating a rigorous verification of the accuracy and soundness of the evaluation results. Finally, we conduct systematic benchmarking on current state-of-the-art methods and provide an in-depth analysis of the limitations present in existing models.

中文摘要翻译: 近期，文生图生成模型展现了从文本输入合成高度逼真图像的卓越能力。尽管现有基准能在一定程度上评估各种模型的生成能力，但它们难以全面、准确地从多个维度衡量模型性能，且常常无法揭示模型在特定类别中的固有缺陷。为解决上述局限性，我们提出了WeGenBench——一个面向文生图生成能力全面多角度评估的新型基准。该基准共包含4000个测试提示，涵盖两大主要类别，并精心平衡中英文分布，以评估双语及跨文化生成能力。在宏观场景分类之外，我们针对每种语言的不同内容和挑战，为每个提示标注了多维标签，从而将生成任务细化为更具体的子类别。通过融合场景分类与多维标签的跨维度评估机制，WeGenBench能够精准定位模型在特定生成类别中的不足。此外，为了更准确地衡量生成质量，我们通过集成视觉-语言模型（VLM），设计并验证了多个新颖的评估指标，从三个核心方面评估模型在领域特定任务上的表现。关键在于，我们的方法不仅输出评估结果，还提供详细的推理轨迹，从而便于对评估结果的准确性和合理性进行严格验证。最后，我们对当前最先进的方法进行了系统性的基准测试，并深入分析了现有模型中存在的局限性。

</details>


### [58] [Geometry-Preserving in 3D Gaussian Splatting for LiDAR-Camera Extrinsic Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20103)
*Kyoleen Kwak,Daeho Kim,Jeong Woon Lee,Hyoseok Hwang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种保持高斯代理度量几何的框架，通过聚合多视角LiDAR观测进行密集深度监督，并阻止光度梯度更新高斯空间参数，从而在目标less方法中提升LiDAR-相机标定精度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于3DGS的目标less标定方法因3DGS原始设计用于新视角合成，倾向于优先渲染质量，导致代理几何偏离真实LiDAR结构，限制了标定精度。

Method: 聚合多视角LiDAR观测生成密集深度监督，阻止光度梯度更新高斯空间参数，从而保持高斯代理的度量几何。

Result: 在公开驾驶数据集上，该方法在标定精度上持续优于现有目标less方法。

Conclusion: 通过保留高斯代理的度量几何，有效提升了LiDAR-相机标定的准确性，优于现有无目标方法。

Original Abstract: Accurate LiDAR-camera calibration is essential for robust multi-modal perception. Targetless approaches avoid manual setup but remain limited by the scarcity of discriminative cross-modal features. Recent methods address this by reconstructing the scene within a differentiable model, enabling extrinsic optimization through dense photometric supervision. Among these, 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has been widely adopted as a geometric proxy that bridges LiDAR and camera within a single differentiable framework. However, since 3DGS was originally designed for novel view synthesis, existing methods tend to prioritize rendering quality, causing the proxy geometry to drift from the true LiDAR structure. We propose a framework that preserves the metric geometry of the Gaussian proxy by aggregating multi-view LiDAR observations for dense depth supervision and blocking photometric gradients from updating the Gaussian spatial parameters. We validate our method on public driving datasets, where it consistently outperforms existing targetless methods in calibration accuracy.

中文摘要翻译: 准确的LiDAR-相机标定对于鲁棒的多模态感知至关重要。无目标方法避免了手动设置，但仍受限于缺乏判别性的跨模态特征。最近的方法通过在可微分模型中重建场景，实现基于密集光度监督的外参优化。其中，3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）被广泛采用作为几何代理，在单一可微分框架内桥接LiDAR和相机。然而，由于3DGS最初是为新视角合成设计的，现有方法倾向于优先渲染质量，导致代理几何偏离真实的LiDAR结构。我们提出一个框架，通过聚合多视角LiDAR观测进行密集深度监督，并阻止光度梯度更新高斯空间参数，从而保持高斯代理的度量几何。我们在公开驾驶数据集上验证了该方法，其在标定精度上持续优于现有无目标方法。

</details>


### [59] [EFIQA: Explainable Fundus Image Quality Assessment via Anatomical Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20108)
*Pengwei Wang,José Morano,Qian Wan,Hrvoje Bogunović*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EFIQA提出了一种无需质量标签的眼底图像质量评估框架，通过解剖先验的无监督异常检测生成空间质量图，在外部数据集上超越监督方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于深度学习的图像质量评估方法依赖于数据集特定的质量标签，导致泛化能力受限且无法提供空间质量反馈，缺乏可解释性。

Method: 两步法：(1) 通过掩膜解剖修复训练无监督异常检测器，识别血管缺失区域；(2) 将先验知识蒸馏到浅层适配器中，将冻结基础模型的特征映射为精确质量图。

Result: 在外部数据集评估中，该无标签方法在最少适配下，性能与可解释性均优于不同质量标准的监督方法。

Conclusion: 无监督解剖先验结合蒸馏方法可实现无需质量标签的高性能可解释图像质量评估，具有实际应用潜力。

Original Abstract: Image quality control is vital for a wide range of downstream applications. Deep learning-based image quality assessment methods typically train classifiers on dataset-specific quality labels, inheriting two limitations: (1) generalization is tied to the labeling criteria of the training set and (2) these methods cannot provide spatial feedback on where the quality is degraded, lacking explainability. In this work, we propose EFIQA, a framework that requires no quality-related supervision and produces spatial quality maps by design. Rather than learning ``what is degradation" from human-annotated labels, EFIQA learns ``what should be there" by leveraging anatomical priors. For fundus photography, we instantiate this as a two-stage approach, by first training an unsupervised anomaly detector via masked anatomical inpainting to identify regions of missing vasculature, and then distilling this prior knowledge into a shallow adapter mapping features of a frozen foundation model to precise quality maps. External-dataset evaluation demonstrates that this label-free approach with minimal adaptation achieves better performance and explainability compared with supervised methods across benchmarks with different quality criteria, highlighting its potential for real-world applications.

中文摘要翻译: 图像质量控制对广泛的下游应用至关重要。基于深度学习的图像质量评估方法通常利用数据集特定的质量标签训练分类器，这继承了两种局限：(1) 泛化能力受限于训练集的标注标准；(2) 这些方法无法提供质量退化位置的空间反馈，缺乏可解释性。本文提出EFIQA，一个无需质量相关监督且通过设计即可生成空间质量图的框架。EFIQA并非从人工标注中学习"什么是退化"，而是通过利用解剖先验学习"那里应该有什么"。针对眼底摄影，我们将其实例化为两阶段方法：首先通过掩膜解剖修复训练一个无监督异常检测器，以识别血管缺失区域；然后将该先验知识蒸馏到一个浅层适配器中，该适配器将冻结的基础模型特征映射为精确的质量图。外部数据集评估表明，这种无标签方法在最少适配下，在具有不同质量标准的基准测试中实现了比监督方法更好的性能和可解释性，突显了其在现实应用中的潜力。

</details>


### [60] [FrozenDrive: Zero-Shot Text-Guided Driving Scene Generation and Data Augmentation with Parameter-Free Frozen Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20110)
*Yuhwan Jeong,Hyeonseong Kim,Daehyun We,Seonkyu Song,Jinnyeong Yang,Hyun-Kurl Jang,Youngho Yoon,Kuk-Jin Yoon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FrozenDrive是一种无需微调预训练扩散模型即可生成多视角、时序一致且高保真自动驾驶场景的框架，尤其在恶劣天气和罕见场景下表现优异，并有效提升了AD模型的鲁棒性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型的自动驾驶场景生成方法存在三个主要问题：1) 为保持多视角和时序一致性而微调骨干网络或添加额外层，会破坏预训练知识并削弱文本对齐能力；2) 生成结果局限于训练分布，难以应对恶劣天气和未见配置；3) 保真度偏向高频类别而忽视稀有类别。FrozenDrive旨在弥补这些空白。
